⾦：其中  Nvidia H100 租⽤價格由每⼩時  US$3.85 調升  16.9% ⾄
US$4.50 ；次世代旗艦  B300 算⼒租⾦更⼤漲  21% 從每⼩時 US$7.85 調升
到  US$9.50 。除此以外， Nebius 也調⾼部分 AMD/Intel x86 CPU 租⽤價格
達 25% ，及存儲記憶體租⽤價格達 41% ， Nebius 同步確認其簽約容量全速朝
向年底  5 GW ⽬標邁進。同⽇， CoreWeave 官⽅宣布⾃  6 ⽉底以來持續以
更⾼價格簽約算⼒，其第三季度簽訂的  3 ⾄  6 個⽉短期客戶合約，年化定價

達到每  MW 約  4,000 萬美元（折合  1 GW 算⼒租⽤合約每年⾼達  400 億美
元），合約總⽤電容量⾃  3.7 GW 增⾄  4.2 GW 。為⽀撐龐⼤建置，
CoreWeave 同步啟動  30 億美元可轉換優先票據發售（另附  5 億美元超額配
售權），並設⽴⾼達  3,500 萬股（約  29.2 億美元）的市價增發（ ATM ）融資
機制。

## Document 036: 2026年8月金融数据点评：8月贷款继续“降速提质”，贷款和政府债券融资拖累社融同比少增

Source PDF: all report pdf/2026年8月金融数据点评：8月贷款继续“降速提质”，贷款和政府债券融资拖累社融同比少增.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

1 
8 月贷款继续“降速提质”，贷款和政府债券融资拖累社融同比少增 
——2026 年 8 月金融数据点评 
王青  冯琳 
事件：2026 年 9 月 14 日，央行公布的数据显示，2026 年 8 月新增人民币贷款600 亿，同比少增5300
亿；8 月新增社会融资规模为 16577 亿，同比少增9083 亿；8 月末，广义货币(M2)同比增长 7.5%，增速较
上月末低0.2 个百分点；狭义货币(M1)同比增长4.1%，增速较上月末高0.1 个百分点。 
基本观点： 
受季节性因素等影响，8 月新增信贷款回正，但规模仅为600 亿，同比少增5300 亿，低于市场普遍预
期。背后是当前城投、地产行业持续处于调整过程，信贷需求下降，而增速较高的高科技行业对信贷依赖
度相对较低。这些都会导致新投放贷款需求减少。另外，当前债市收益率下行，企业债券融资对银行贷款
形成替代，再加上近期企业中长期贷款到期规模明显上升，8000 亿新型政策性金融工具对配套贷款的拉动
作用尚未充分显现等，也是 8 月新增人民币贷款出现较大规模同比少增的直接原因。不过，近期贷款减速
的同时，支持实体经济的质效在提升。其中，8 月包括科技贷款、普惠小微贷款在内的金融“五篇大文章”
贷款保持两位数增长，增量占各项贷款增量的比重提高至70%以上；另外，8 月新发放企业贷款加权平均利
率略低于 3%，比上年同期低约 0.2 个百分点，持续处在历史低位。主要受当月新增贷款较大规模同比少增，
再加上政府债券到期量偏高导致当月政府债券融资也出现同比少增等影响，8 月新增社融同比少增约 9000
亿。后期伴随政府债券发行节奏加快，净融资规模显著上升，新增社融有望恢复同比多增。8 月末 M2 增速
小幅下行，主要源于当月新增贷款规模较低，拖累存款派生。当前M2 增速仍然明显高于名义GDP 增速，显
示货币政策保持支持性立场；8 月末M1 增速略有加快，主要原因是上年同期基数走低。 
展望未来，7 月 30 日中央政治局会议要求，下半年要“加大逆周期调节力度”，“综合运用并适时调
整货币政策工具”。我们预计，央行有可能将在三季度末前后实施降息降准。这是年底前促消费、稳投资，
着力提振内需的一个重要发力点，对稳定房地产市场也有重要意义。

具体来看： 
一、8 月贷款总量延续“降速提质”过程，同比少增主因企业融资需求减弱与居民部门持续降杠

### Page 2

2
杆。但经济加速转型正带动融资结构深层次调整，贷款增长已难以完整反映实体融资全貌；且从投向
看，当前金融“五篇大文章”领域贷款持续高增，有力支撑了经济结构向新向优。
8 月新增人民币贷款600 亿，环比季节性多增4000 亿，但同比少增5300 亿，贷款总量表现依然较弱。
受同比较大幅度少增影响，8 月末各项贷款余额同比增速较上月末放缓 0.2 个百分点至 4.9%，续创有数据
记录以为最低水平。
从分项来看，8 月新增企业贷款2600 亿，同比少增3300 亿。其中，8 月新增企业中长期贷款3200 亿，
同比少增1500 亿。企业中长期贷款表现持续较弱，一方面符合新旧动能转换加速推进的趋势——新动能融
资密度较低，对信贷资金的依赖度小于旧动能；另一方面，也因当前市场需求偏弱，企业投资扩产意愿相
对不足，中长期贷款需求相应减弱。此外，企业中长期贷款还受到以下阶段性因素拖累：一是 银行在
2020-2023 年显著加大信贷投放力度以支持企业纾困，其中中长期贷款占比相对较高，今年迎来到期高峰；

二是企业债券发行利率下行推动融资结构“以债替贷”；三是 8 月政府债券发行温和、政策性金融工具尚
未大规模投放，对配套贷款的撬动作用有限。短贷方面，8 月新增企业短贷-1600 亿，同比多减 2300 亿，
进一步反映企业信贷融资需求整体较弱；同时，8 月新增票据融资 1000 亿，同比多增 469 亿，对企业短贷
也形成一定的替代效应。
居民贷款方面，8 月新增居民贷款-2029 亿，同比多减2332 亿，其中，居民短贷和居民中长期贷款同
比分别多减 1324 亿和 1022 亿。这表明，当前居民部门仍处在降杠杆以修复资产负债表的过程中，这主要
受房地产市场持续调整影响。同时，居民消费意愿不足、个体经营活动复苏乏力等也在抑制居民新增贷款
需求。
总体来看，8 月新增信贷总量依然偏弱，但当前贷款增长已难以完整反映融资增长全貌，背后是伴随
经济加速转型，融资结构正经历深层次调整，直接融资重要性上升，贷款“降速提质”渐成常态。
从投向上看，当前银行体系正顺应经济转型趋势，推动信贷资源更多流向科技创新、绿色转型等新动

能领域。根据央行数据，8 月末金融“五篇大文章”领域贷款保持两位数快速增长，增量占各项贷款增量
的比重已提高至 70%以上；普惠小微贷款余额、不含房地产业的服务业中长期贷款余额同比分别增长 8.3%
和 9.3%，均高于全部贷款增速，信贷投放结构持续向新向优。从利率上看，8 月企业新发放贷款加权平均
利率略低于3%，同比低约0.2 个百分点；个人住房新发放贷款加权平均利率约3.1%，与上年同期持平——
贷款利率继续保持低位运行，反映信贷资金供给较为充裕，正持续助力实体经济降本增效。
二、受当月贷款投放规模偏低，政府债券融资同比少增影响，8 月社融同比少增约 9000 亿，但

### Page 3

3
当月企业债券融资同比继续多增，直接融资渠道重要性上升。
8 月新增社融1.66 万亿，环比多增2509 亿，但同比大幅少增9083 亿，拖累月末社融存量同比增
速较上月末下滑0.2 个百分点至7.2%。
从分项来看，8 月社融同比少增，主要受投向实体经济的人民币贷款和政府债券融资拖累。其中，
投向实体经济的人民币贷款同比少增 5701 亿，政府债券融资同比少增 3575 亿。需要说明的是，8 月
新增政府债券融资 1.01 万亿，绝对规模并不低，同比大幅少增主要源于去年同期基数偏高；同时，
当月政府债券到期量同比明显增多，净融资同比相应下滑。此外，8 月票据融资同比多增，带动表外
票据转表内，当月表外票据融资同比少增1591 亿，以及信托贷款延续压降，同比多减583 亿，也是社
融同比大幅少增的原因。
社融其他分项中，8 月债券发行利率继续下行，提振企业债券融资同比多增1374 亿；同时，8 月
非金融企业境内股票融资同比多增 183 亿，连续第 7 个月同比多增。当前债券、股票等直接融资渠道

的重要性上升，尤其在支持科技型企业融资方面发挥重要作用。此外，8 月外币贷款同比多增 500 亿，
委托贷款同比多增 394 亿，对当月社融同比起到一定支撑作用。值得一提的是，9 月以来新增政策性
金融工具加速投放，且多以委托贷款渠道落地，预计新增委托贷款将明显放量，并撬动相关配套贷款
需求，这将对后续社融和信贷形成支撑。另外据我们测算，9-12 月政府债券融资将同比多增约1.5 万
亿，这将带动后期社融恢复同比多增。
三、8 月末 M2 增速小幅下行，主要源于当月新增贷款规模较低，拖累存款派生。当前 M2 增速仍
然明显高于名义GDP 增速，显示货币政策保持支持性立场；8 月末M1 增速略有加快，主要原因是上年
同期基数走低。当前 M1 增速偏低，与 M2 增速之间存在明显的“剪刀差”，背后是内需偏弱背景下，
宽货币向宽信用传导不畅。
8 月末 M2 同比增速为 7.5%，较上月末放缓 0.2 个百分点。主要原因是当月新增贷款仅为600 亿，
同比大幅少增5300 亿，拖累存款派生。总体来看，当前M2、存量社融增速运行在7.0%-8.0%区间，仍显著

高于上半年 5.4%的名义 GDP 增速，表明当前社会融资条件处在较为宽松的状态，货币政策保持支持性立场。
8 月末 M1 同比增速为 4.1%，较上月末小幅加快 0.1 个百分点，主要原因是去年同期 M1 环比增速为
0.15%，显著低于过去十年1.1%的平均环比增速。这意味着2025 年 8 月 M1 基数偏低，会推高今年 8 月 M1
同比增速。当前 M1 增速处于偏低水平，背后是内需偏弱背景下，企业和居民的投资、消费活跃度较低。8
月末 M2 与 M1 增速“剪刀差”为 3.4 个百分点，较上月收窄 0.3 个百分点，继续处于偏高状态。背后的主

### Page 4

4
要原因在于，房地产市场持续调整导致投资消费动能不足，资金活化程度偏低，宽货币向宽信用传导存在
阻滞。这意味着后续宏观政策需要持续发力提振内需，关键是要切实稳定房地产市场。
展望未来，7 月 30 日中央政治局会议要求，下半年要“加大逆周期调节力度”，“综合运用并适时调
整货币政策工具”。这意味着接下来货币政策在总量和结构性政策工具方面都有发力空间， 可能出台的措
施包括：一是结构性货币政策工具继续降价、增量、扩围，加大对科技融资、普惠金融的定向支持力度，
推动新旧动能转换，提升金融服务实体经济质效。二是央行择机实施降息降准，估计降息幅度为 10 个基
点，降准 0.5 个百分点。这是年底前促消费、稳投资，着力提振内需的一个重要发力点，对稳定房地产市
场也有重要意义。综合考虑经济运行态势及物价走势，预计三季度末前后以上增量政策有可能落地。

聯電 (2303 TT)  Buy

中長期業績展望佳，建議 Buy。  
投資建議 ：展望 2026 年， AI 基礎設施的需求正帶動整體市場
復甦，並外溢至記憶體、網通與電源管理領域，然消費性電子
(如手機、 PC) 終端需求仍偏弱，且庫存天數有微幅上升的跡象。
儘管如此，聯電預期其 2026 年整體晶圓出貨量仍將成長，特別
是在 22/28 nm 與 8 吋的市占率持續提升。聯電預估 2026 年 AI
相關營收將近 3 億美元，並預期 3 年內突破 10 億美元大關。預
估聯電 2026 年稅後 EPS 7.72 元。公司在先進封裝 (interposer 或
矽電容等 )、矽光子及 Intel 合作的 12nm 陸續取得進展，為 2027
年後中長期成長奠定基礎。雖聯電 2Q26 獲利大多以投資收益
為主， 3Q26 股市成長動能相對弱，可能壓抑業外收益，但本業
獲利逐步回升，建議 Buy ，目標價 169 元。  
2Q26 稅後 EPS 3.36 元：聯電 2Q26 營收為 687.33 億元，
QoQ+12.61% ，整體出貨量約當  12 吋晶圓 112.90 萬片，
QoQ+10.58% 。 2Q26 產能利用率增加至  85% 。 ASP 穩定向上。
2Q26 產能利用率增至 85% ， ASP QoQ 小增。 2Q26 毛利率增加
3.28 個百分點至 32.48% 。業外認列  302.36 億元收益，包含利
息收益  2.18 億元、匯兌收益 0.03 億元，認列投資收益  300.45 
億元 (1Q26 則為  49.97 億元投資收益 )。聯電 2Q26 稅後 EPS 
3.36 元 。 若不計算投資收益 ，2Q26 稅後 EPS 0.97 元，QoQ+9.31% 。 
預估 3Q26 稅後 EPS 1.47 元：預期 3Q26 在電腦、通訊及消費
性領域的需求動能將保持穩定。出貨成長主要由電源管理  IC 、
感測器及微控制器（ MCU ）的強勁需求所推動。 8 吋晶圓產品
線正迎來強勁反彈，預期 3Q26 產能利用率將有顯著改善；而  
12 吋產能目前已處於健康的利用率水位，足以支撐核心業務發
展。預 估 聯 電3Q26 晶圓出貨量 QoQ+7~9% ， 產能利用率約 90%。
ASP in USD QoQ+0% ，不過 2026 年的定價環境更具優勢。這主
要源於市場供需關係的結構性改善，毛利率增至約 35% 左右。
預估聯電 3Q26 稅後 EPS 1.47 元。

2 群益投顧 
  聯電 ESG ：  
聯電在 ESG 方面持續展現積極作為，並獲國內外多項評鑑肯定：  
永續策略與治理： ESG 由董事會與二位共同總經理組成的指導委員會全盤
規劃，持續以 「以人為本、與環境共生、與社會共榮」 為願景推動永續。 ESG
績效結合員工分紅與限制型股票條件，提升全員參與動能與企業凝聚力。  
環境面（ E）  
2025 年獲國際永續評鑑（如 FTSE Russell ）評為全台 ESG 表現頂尖企業，
半導體業唯一入選前 3。  
持續推動用水回收、能源效率提升，製程用水回收率在各廠均高於法規標
準。  
積極採購並投資離岸風電，與 CIP 渢妙風場簽 30 年綠電採購合約，展現對
2050 淨零承諾。  
社會面（ S）  
深化產學合作，與台灣 4 所大學半導體專班合作， 600 多位學生參與。  
啟動員工持股信託，落實多元薪酬政策，提升員工福祉並吸引人才。  
長期投入社會公益，如偏鄉關懷、青年培力、綠色創新等持續進行。  
公司治理（ G）  
董事會組成高度多元且獨立，獨董占三分之二、女性董事占三分之一。  
強化利害關係人溝通與資訊透明度，持續強化供應鏈 ESG 要求，並納入評
核與管理系統，推動價值鏈共榮。

6 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

「買進」，目標價: $2,000

報告日期：2026.09.16 研 究 員：邱岳霖

摘 要 及 調 整 原 因 
➢ 缺料影響營運不如預期，下修 Q3獲利預估，EPS 9元 
➢ HVDC滲透率提升，且持續海內外擴產，2027 年營收挑戰超越 1兆元，EPS 62.3元。考量
電源產業將進入全新格局且台達作為主要先行者將最為受惠，兆豐投顧維持目標價 2000 元
(約 32X 27(F)PER)，潛在上漲空間 19.8%，建議逢低買進。

基本資料 結論與建議

- 2 - 
個股報告 
資料來源：CMoney；兆豐投顧預估

營 運 分 析

台達電為全球電源管理及散熱解決方案領導廠商 
台達電(2308)成立於 1971 年並於 1988 年掛牌上市，成立初期分別投入生產
交換式電源供應器與無碳刷式直流風扇，目 前已取得全球電源產品龍頭地
位。2017 年公司啟動組織調整，以「電源及零組件」、「自動化」、「基礎
設施」、「交通」四大業務範疇，並從傳統關鍵零組件製造商逐步轉型為節
能解決方案提供者。近年隨AI 資料中心建置需求爆發，公司積極將既有電源
技術延伸至 AI伺服器電源機櫃、液冷 side car、BBU、DC-DC/AC-DC模組
及 CDU 等新產品線，目前終端應用已從傳統 NB/工業電源、資料中心跨足
至高壓供電(HVDC)與液冷散熱系統，客戶涵蓋NVIDIA與北美 CSP及 ASIC
供應鏈。

26Q2 營收比重:(1)電源及零組件 53%,包含電源、被動元件等零組件、風扇
散熱;(2)交通 5%,電動車解決方案;(3)自動化 8%,包含工業自動化,樓宇自動
化;(4)基礎設施 34%,包含資訊通訊技術(通信電源、網通交換器、資料中心
解決方案、液冷side car)、能源基礎設施(電動車充電樁、可再生能源及儲能
設備等)。受惠 AI 資料中心電源需求與規格持續提升，台達持續擴充台灣、
中國大陸、泰國與美國產能，並同步推進 800V HVDC 架構開發，全面迎接
NVIDIA 新世代 GPU 機櫃功耗大幅提升所帶動的電源與散熱升級需求。

- 3 - 
個股報告

缺料影響營運不如預期，下修 Q3獲利預估，EPS 9 元 
台達電(2308)公告 8月營收：營收 645.9 億元，MoM -3.7%/YoY +34.9%，
Q3達成率 62.4%，低於研究部預期與市場共識，係因 MOSFET 全產品線呈
現缺料所致，故研究部下修台達電 26Q3 營收 1960.8 億元(原估 2,107.4 億
元)，QoQ +7.0%/YoY +30.4%，而先前 18.3KW PSU 因電容問題有進行產
品召回，不過預期本月將處理完畢且對財務無任何影響；毛利率方面，受惠
產品組合轉佳與規模經濟抵銷原料/電子元件漲價影響，預估毛利率 35.8%，
仍可維持與上季相當，季增 0.2ppt，反映營收下修，同步下修稅後淨利 232.6
億元，QoQ -7.5%/YoY +25.0%，EPS 9元(原估 10.5 元)。

AI 電源與散熱產品持續放量，輔以新產品推出，2026 EPS 37元 
展望 2026 年，主要成長動能仍來自 AI 資料中心電源、散熱與基礎設施，在
電源方面隨著 GB300 持續放量與 H2 VR200之 18.3KW 產品開始出貨，預
期價量齊揚挹注營運動能，此外 HVDC(±400V/800V)產品於 3Q26 開始量
產，惟在初期階段預期僅小幅貢獻；在散熱產品方面(涵蓋水冷板、Manifold、
CDU及 Sidecar)在 2026 下半年可望延續成長動能，主要受惠液冷滲透率持
續提升與內容價值同步墊高。綜上所述，反映 Q3下修，同步下修台達電 2026
年營運預估：2026 年營收 7,465.4 億元，YoY +34.5%，毛利率 36.2%，年
增 1.9ppt，稅後淨利 960.8 億元，YoY +59.8%，EPS 37.0元（原估 40 元）。

HVDC滲透率提升，且持續海內外擴產，2027年營收挑戰超越 1兆元，EPS 
62.3元 
展望 2027 年，受惠 CSP業者 CAPEX 持續增長，公司為滿足訂單需求，持
續加大投資力道，2026 年資本支出約 700 億元(YoY +50%)，資金來源除自
有資金外，9/15 公告發行泰達電可交換公司債籌措 15 億美元以滿足擴產資
金需求，2027 年也可望維持高檔水準，預計於泰國、中國、台灣與美國同步
擴產，研究部認為 HVDC(±400V/800V)較大規模量產貢獻有望於 2027 年起
顯現，同時液冷、電源機櫃、DC-DC模組等新產品線持續放量，此外公司亦
持續推進 SST 與 SOFC 等能源基礎設施長期布局，電源產業將迎來新格局
與長期成長動能。兆豐投顧預估台達電 2027 年營收 1.1 兆元，YoY +43.0%，
毛利率 37%，年增 0.8ppt，稅後淨利 1617.6 億元，YoY +68.4%，EPS 62.3
元。

ab 14 September 2026
Global Research
First Read
WIN Semiconductors
2026 Taiwan Summit: Optical and Infrastructure
to drive mid-to-long-term growth
Q326 revenue tracking in line with guidance
WIN Semi attended our 2026 Taiwan Summit on Sep 14th. August revenue of NT
$1,937mn (+2% QoQ and +31%YoY) is tracking at 65% of both UBSe/Street at +12%
QoQ. WIN Semi maintained its Q326F revenue guidance of low-teens QoQ  growth,
mainly driven by optical and infrastructure segments. It noted revenue outlook from

WiFi has improved from a previously flattish QoQ  to sequentially QoQ growth as a result
of stronger router demand, and cellular remains flattish. Management did not provide
Q426 guidance, noting that demand will partly depend on smartphone seasonality,
although it remains more constructive on the growth outlook for aerospace and non-
smartphone optical markets. In terms of  capex, WIN Semi budgeted NT$2-3bn of capex
spending in 2026, primarily for GaN and InP expansion.

Laser diode remains in qualification but photodiode ramp accelerates
Management noted that its CW lasers and EML remains under  qualification with more
meaningful revenue contribution from 2027 and beyond. While WIN Semi is engaging
with a broader customer base for laser diode than for photodiode, management noted
its near-term optical growth should be driven primarily by the ongoing photodiode
ramp, with volumes expected to increase sequentially both MoM and QoQ through

H226F and continue strengthening into 2027F. Management also noted that its 50G
and 100G VCSEL solutions are already in production, providing additional support for
non-smartphone optical growth. As a result, smartphone-related optical is expected to
decline from 60-70% of optical revenue historically to around 50% in Q326F as non-
smartphone optical applications expand. Management maintained its target for AI/
datacom revenue (driver, PIC, laser diode, photodiode, and etc) exposure to reach high-

single digits by end-2026, up from a low-single-digit contribution in 2025.
Rising aerospace content strengthens long-term growth outlook
Management remains constructive on the long-term outlook and its margin profile as
the business mix shifts toward higher-margin optical and aerospace applications.
Aerospace revenue has increased to over 30% of infrastructure revenue (Infrastructure
was 30-35% of total sales in Q226F), driven by expanding LEO deployments, direct-to-

cell connectivity, and rising RF content as additional frequency bands and advanced
phased-array antennas are adopted, as well as migration from GaAs to GaN platform.
Management views aerospace and optical datacom as key medium- to long-term
growth drivers, supporting a more diversified revenue mix and improved margin profile.
Valuation: Maintain Buy rating with a PE-based price target of NT$500
We rate WIN Semi with  BUY and  PT of NT$500, based on 40x 2027-28E average PE. We

believe WIN Semi is well positioned to capture rising infrastructure and optical growth.

Company Description
Established in 1999, WIN Semiconductors is a leading foundry for compound semiconductor
wafers globally, with 43k 6-inch wafer capacity per month as of end-2022. While it offers
different types of compound semiconductors (GaAs, GaN, InP, etc.), the GaAs compound
contributes the majority of its revenue. Its products are used to make signal power amplifiers

that customers use in networking equipment and communication devices, including base
stations and smartphones.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
We value WIN Semi using PE relative to its historical trading range and peer average.
Investing in WIN Semi involves risks that include, but are not limited to, rapid technological
changes, increasing competition, exposure to macroeconomic cycles and uncertain
enddemand. Slower wireless technology migration and poor performances by customers

could also significantly affect WIN Semi’s fundamental performance. WIN Semi’s LEO satellite
customers may face execution challenges in launching satellites, which will impact WIN
Semi's infrastructure revenue growth outlook. On the other hand, its progress on developing
and qualifying the optical communication laser diodes will have significant impact to its
earnings outlook and market sentiment. Moreover, it is extremely difficult to project the

financial results of tech companies, since their operating models are highly volatile and
unpredictable.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.
Important Information About Sustainable Investing Strategies: Sustainable investing strategies aim to consider and incorporate environmental, social and governance
(ESG) factors into investment process and portfolio construction. Strategies across geographies approach ESG analysis and incorporate the findings in a variety of ways.
Incorporating ESG factors or sustainable investing considerations may inhibit the portfolio manager’s ability to participate in certain investment opportunities that otherwise

would be consistent with its investment objective and other principal investment strategies. The returns on a portfolio incorporating ESG factors or sustainable investing
considerations may be lower or higher than portfolios where ESG factors, exclusions, or other sustainability issues are not considered by the portfolio manager, and the
investment opportunities available to such portfolios may differ.

• August revenue was NT$1,056mn (+5% m/m, +60% y/y). August +
September revenues account for 70% of our 3Q revenue estimate and 66%
of the Street's.
• August pre-tax profit was NT$254mn (+123% y/y), 34% of our 3Q pre-tax
profit estimate and 28% of the Street's.
• August net profit was NT$203mnM (+123% y/y), 34%/28% of MSe/Street's

3Q net profit estima
• August pre-tax profit was NT$254mn (+123% y/y), 34% of our 3Q pre-tax
profit estimate and 28% of the Street's.
We remain constructive on Co-Tech: We expect the undersupply of  HVLP4 copper
foil to persist into CY27-28. See our recent initiation report). We believe this should
support more proactive price hikes by Co-Tech, which should drive further margin
expansion. We reiterate our OW rating with a NT$730 price target.

Key Takeaways
Co-Tech's August net profit was in line with our estimate but below Street
consensus.
August net margin of 19.2% was slightly below the 2Q net margin of 19.4%, as
well as our 3Q net margin estimate of 20.2% and the Street at 23.0%.
We believe the softer margin was likely driven by higher summer electricity rates
(May 16 to October 15 for Co-Tech) and elevated input costs.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -

8462 柏文(Fitness Factory)

報告日期： 2026/09/16 研 究 員：洪  國 峰 (Titan Horng)  
壹、投 資 建 議

一. 預估 3Q26 稅後 EPS 3.26 元 
柏文 07~08/2026 營收金額 6.26 億元與 6.22 億元， YoY+18.28%
與 16.75% ，MoM+4.03% 與-0.56% ，預估柏文 3Q26 營收金額
18.78 億元， YoY+17.41% ，QoQ+5.80% ，預估營業利益 3.50 億
元， YoY+51.34% ， QoQ+4.3% ，預估稅後盈餘 2.62 億元，
YoY+55.65% ，QoQ+1.02% ，預估稅後 EPS 3.26 元。

二. 預估 4Q26 稅後 EPS 3.53 元 
在新店集中在 4Q26 開幕，預估柏文 4Q26 營收金額 19.2 億元，
YoY+17.3% ， QoQ+2.28% ，預估營業利益 3.81 億元，
YoY+34.35% ， QoQ+8.92% ，預估稅後盈餘 2.84 億元，
YoY+29.01% ，QoQ+8.39% ，預估稅後 EPS 3.53 元。

三. 預計 2026 年稅後 EPS 12.40 元 
柏文 2026 年在同店成長與新點動能雙引擎帶動下 ， 單點會員人數
將會到疫情前水準，帶動柏文 2026 年營運持續成長。預估柏文
2026 年營收金額 72.00 億元， YoY+18.7% ，預估稅後盈餘 9.97
億元， YoY+49.68% ，預估稅後 EPS 12.40 元。

四. 預估 2027 年稅後 EPS 15.60 元 
柏文 2027 年在「新店動能」、「同店成長」及「會員二次消費增
加」三項成長動能帶動柏文 2027 年營收成長 ， 獲利方面受惠2026
年漲價效應逐漸顯現，獲利成長幅度將優於營收成長幅度。預估
柏文 2027 年營收金額 81.5 億元， YoY+13.2% ，預估稅後盈餘
12.54 億元， YoY+25.82% ，預估稅後 EPS 15.60 元。

五. 投資建議買進，目標價 200 元 
在疫情結束後，國內健身房產業大者恆大，經濟規模效益逐漸顯
現，柏 文2026 年獲利將回升至疫情前水準 ， 觀察柏文2016~2019
年 ROE 20~32% ，PER 14~45X ，柏文 2026 年 ROE39% ，PER 
12.9X ，2027 年 ROE 37% ，PER 10.3X ，評價偏低，加上統一健
身退出市場，柏文藉此吸收統一健身會員，有助評價提升，投資
建議買進，目標價 200 元。（ 16.1X 2026 年稅後 EPS ）

貳、公 司 營 運 概 況

一. 台灣健身房運動市場規模  
根據教育部體育署統計資料， 2025 年全國 15 歲以上平常有運動
人口數在 1717.3 萬人，運動人口比例 83.3% ，其中上健身房使用
器材人 82.4 萬人，占運動人口比重 4.8% ，占全國 15 歲人口比
44% ，相較香港 5.85% 、新加波 5.76% 水準來說，國內健身房運
動人口具成長空間。

3 
二. 公司簡介  
柏文健康事業成立於 2005 年，資本額 8.04 億元， 2016 年掛牌上
櫃，並於 2019 年轉上市。柏文主要經營會員制連鎖運動健身中心
與休閒運動場館，柏文為國內第二大連鎖健身中心，僅次於世界
健身（ World GYM ）。有別於其他同業，柏文遵循定型化契約預
收使用費 50% 信託收費標準化 、 透明化 ， 以減少與客戶消費糾紛，
柏文會員主要以月繳型會員為主 ， 其中月繳制會員高達 85~90% 、
其餘 10~15% 為預繳制會員。採用進口頂級器材、引進創新課程，
以提高月繳型會員續約率，柏文會員續約率高達 70~75% ，優於
歐美地區的 30~35% 水準。

三. 旗下品牌與全台據點概況  
柏文旗下品牌主要有健身工廠、兒童體適能俱樂部、肖跳、滾吧、
人體工房與鐳射戰場等六個事業體，其中主要以健身工廠為營運
主體，若以會費等級區分，主要分旗艦店、中型店與小型店。旗
艦店月費 1688 元(一般金卡會員 )；小型店月費 1088 元(一般金卡
會員 )；中型店月費 1288 元(一般金卡會員 )。

表三、柏文旗下品牌概況

資料來源：公司、宏遠預估與整理

柏文 2025 年增加 8 家據點，分別為新竹竹北廠 (2 月開幕，取代
原本租約到期之竹北廠 )，台南新市廠 (2 月開幕 )，桃園青埔廠 (4

平廠， 2025 年底據點數 83 家。

四. 2026 年營運概況及展望

柏文營收主要分健身及娛樂服務、運動保建服務、入會費收入、
其他等四部分。

預估 2026 年健身及娛樂服務營收金額 44.55 億元， YoY+19.8%

健身及娛樂服務，主要為每月會員繳交月費部分。健身及娛樂服
務 2025 年營收金額 37.18 億元， YoY+20.17% 。健身及娛樂服務
2025 年營收成長動能包括： 1，在會員續約率由 2024 年的 62%
提升至 2025 年目標 65% ，加上 2025 年新增 8~9 家據點，柏文
2025 年底會員人數將來到 35~36 萬人，增幅達 16~20% 。2，柏
文己在 2024 年調整會籍使用費 ，2Q25~3Q25 調整效益開始顯現 。

健身及娛樂服務 01~08/2026 營收金額 28.80 億元 ，YoY+21.06% 。 
預期 2026 年，在會籍使用費效益持續顯現，加上新增據點數持續
增加，推估 2026 年底會員人數有機會來到 42~43 萬人，增幅
15.4~18.1% ，宏遠預估健身及娛樂服務 2026 年營收金額 44.55
億元， YoY+19.8% 。

預估 2026 年運動保健服務營收金額 25.1 億元， YoY+17.3%

運動保健服務，主要為會員再付費的教練課程，柏文會員人數中
約當 18~20% 會員會購買教練課程。

運動保健服務 2025 年年營收金額 21.4 億元， YoY+14.84% 。運
動保健服務 2025 年營運成長動能包括： 1，柏文 2024 年己開始
調整教練課程售價， 2025 年開始顯現。 2，柏文推出一對二與二
對三等，小型團體教練課程，將有助提升教練課程在會員滲透率
提升。 3，健身工廠針對樂齡者推出超高益處的「茁健活力班」，
且不限會員參加，深耕銀髮族群，將有助柏文拓展樂齡非會員族
群。4，在運動按摩業務方面，柏文旗下己有少部分門市推出，小
幅貢獻 2025 年營收。

預估 2026 年入會費營收金額 1.50 億元， YoY+5.6%

入會費收入 ， 係指首次加入健身工廠會員所支付費用 。 入會費2023
年營收金額 1.15 億元， YoY-23.2% 。

入會費 2024 年營收金額 1.18 億元， YoY+2.87% ，回顧 2024 年
新增據點數將較 2023 年多一家 ， 加上以近期新增據點招收會員情
況來觀察，已有回升至疫情前 85~90% 水準。 2025 年在新增據點
8~9 家，相 較2024 年多一家 ，2025 年入會費營收金額 1.42 億元 ，
YoY+20.34% 。預估 2026 年入會費營收金額 1.50 億元，
YoY+5.63% 。預 估2027 年入會費營收金額 1.50 億元 ，YoY+0.0%。

叁、業 績 及 獲 利 預 估

預估 2026 年稅後盈餘 9.97 億元， YoY+49.68% ，預估稅後 EPS 
12.40 元

柏文 2026 年在同店成長與新點動能雙引擎帶動下 ， 單點會員人數
將會到疫情前水準，帶動柏文 2026 年營運持續成長。預估柏文
2026 年營收金額 72.00 億元， YoY+18.7% ，預估營業利益 13.29
億元， YoY+47.6% ，預估稅後盈餘 9.97 億元， YoY+49.68% ，預
估稅後 EPS 12.40 元。

預估 2027 年稅後盈餘 12.54 億元，YoY+25.82% ，預估稅後 EPS 
15.60 元

柏文 2027 年在「新店動能」、「同店成長」及「會員二次消費增
加」三項成長動能帶動柏文 2027 年營收成長 ， 獲利方面受惠2026
年漲價效應逐漸顯現，獲利成長幅度將優於營收成長幅度。預估
柏文 2027 年營收金額 81.5 億元， YoY+13.2% ，預估營業利益
16.62 億元， YoY+25.05% ，預估稅後盈餘 12.54 億元，
YoY+25.82% ，預估稅後 EPS 15.60 元。

## Document 157: 8月经济增长数据解读：8月经济运行平稳向新向优

Source PDF: all report pdf/8月经济增长数据解读：8月经济运行平稳向新向优.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

市场研究部 2026年9月15日
事件
9月 15日，国家统计局发布，8月，规模以上工业增加值同比
增长 5.2%，社会消费品零售总额同比增长 0.4%，1-8 月，固定资
产投资同比下降 7.2%。
解读
一、8月工业增加值同比增速小幅提高，高技术制造业增加值
保持高增长。
8月，规模以上工业增加值环比增长 0.54%，比 7月提高 0.43
个百分点，同比增长 5.2%，比 7月提高 0.7个百分点。
1-8月，全国规模以上工业增加值同比增长 5.3%，与 1-7月持
平，比去年 1-8月回落 0.9个百分点。
其中，8 月，制造业增加值同比增长 6.1%，比 7 月提高 0.6
个百分点，采矿业同比下降 1.4%，降幅比 7月收窄 2.8个百分点，
电力热力水的生产供应业同比增长 4.9%，比 7 月回落 0.1 个百分
点。
8 月，高技术制造业增加值同比增长 16.7%，比 7 月回落 0.2
个百分点。
二、1-8 月重要行业增加值 18 个同比增长，8 月 17 个同比增
长。

8月，20个重要行业中，有 17个行业增加值同比增长，其中，
增长速度较高的分别是：
计算机、通信和其他电子设备制造业增加值同比增长 17.2%；
铁路、船舶、航空航天和其他运输设备制造业增加值同比增长
13.4%；
专用设备制造业增加值同比增长 11.4%。
增速同比下降的分别是：
煤炭开采和洗选业增加值同比下降 5.6%；
非金属矿物制品业增加值同比下降 4.1%；
有色金属冶炼和压延加工业增加值同比下降 4.1%。
宏观研究
8月经济运行平稳向新向优
——8月经济增长数据解读
市场表现截至 2026.8.31
资料来源：Wind,国新证券整理。
分析师：郝大明
登记编码：S1490514010002
邮箱：haodaming@crsec.com.cn
证券研究报告

### Page 2

1-8 月，20 个重要行业中，有 18 个行业增加值同比增长，2
个行业同比下降。其中，增长速度较高的是：
计算机、通信和其他电子设备制造业增加值同比增长 15.7%；
铁路、船舶、航空航天和其他运输设备制造业增加值同比增长
12.8%；
专用设备制造业增加值同比增长 9.2%。
增速下降的是：
非金属矿物制品业增加值同比下降 3.0%，
有色金属冶炼和压延加工业增加值同比下降 1.1%。
8 月，20 个重要制造业行业增加值中，11个行业增加值同比
增速高于 7月，1个行业与 7月持平，9 个行业低于 7月，其中，
提高幅度较大的是：
煤炭开采业增加值同比-5.6%，降幅比 7月收窄 5.2个百分点；
医药制造业增加值同比增长 5.2%，比 7月提高 2.4个百分点。
下滑幅度较大的是：
计算机、通信和其他电子设备制造业增加值同比增长 17.2%，
比 7月回落 1.9个百分点；
有色金属冶炼和压延加工业增加值同比-4.1，降幅比 7月扩大
1.6个百分点。
1-8月，10 个行业增加值同比增速高于 1-7 月，8个行业增加

值同比增速低于 1-7月，其中，煤炭开采和洗选业增加值同比增长
0.8%，比 1-7月回落 0.8个百分点，其余行业增加值增速波动非常
小。
三、8 月工业机器人、新能源汽车和集成电路等产品产量较高
增长。
8月，工业机器人产量同比增长 34.6%，比 7月提高 4.4个百
分点；
集成电路产量同比增长20.6%，比 7月回落 0.1个百分点；
汽车产量同比-2.7%，降幅比 7 月扩大 2.6 个百分点，其中轿
车产量同比-22.7%，降幅比 7月扩大 5.6个百分点，新能源汽车产
量同比增长 21.9%，比 7 月回落 8.0 个百分点，SUV 同比增长
17.0%，比 7月提高 3.4个百分点。
服务机器人产量同比增长 5.0%，比 7月回落 0.7个百分点。
发电机组（发电设备）产量同比-10.2%，降幅比 7月收窄 3.7
个百分点。
太阳能电池（光伏电池）产量同比-12.9%，降幅比 7 月扩大
3.5个百分点。

### Page 3

规模以上发电量同比-0.8%，降幅比 7 月扩大 0.7 个百分点，
其中，火电同比-4.3%，降幅比 7 月扩大 0.8 个百分点，太阳能发
电同比增长 10.3%，比 7月提高 4.7个百分点。
微型计算机设备产量同比-25.6%，移动通信手持机产量同比-
18.0%，其中，智能手机产量同比-22.3%。
房地产产业链产品产量均为下降，其中，水泥产量同比-11.7%，
平板玻璃同比-7.6%，生铁产量同比-3.5%，粗钢产量同比-3.7%，
钢材产量同比-5.5%。
另外，化工产业链产品产量多数下降，其中，原油加工量同
比-6.9%，硫酸同比-15.5%，烧碱产量同比-2.3%，化纤产量同比-
3.2%，焦炭产量同比-6.2%。
四、1-8 月固定资产投资同比降幅小幅扩大，8 月固定资产投
资同比降幅小幅收窄。
1-8月，固定资产投资同比下降 7.2%，降幅比 1-7月扩大 0.5
个百分点。其中，基础设施建设投资同比-5.4%，降幅比 1-7 月扩
大 1.3 个百分点，制造业投资同比-2.3%，降幅比 1-7 月扩大 0.6

个百分点，房地产开发投资同比下降 19.7%，降幅比 1-7 月扩大
0.5个百分点。
8 月当月，固定资产投资同比下降 11.0%，降幅比 7 月收窄
1.9 个百分点。其中，房地产开发投资同比下降 25.5%，降幅比 7
月收窄 1.9个百分点，住宅投资同比下降 24.5%，降幅比 7月收窄
3.2个百分点。
五、8 月社会消费品零售总额同比增速比7月小幅回落。
1-8 月，社会消费品零售总额同比增长 1.1%，比 1-7 月回落
0.1 个百分点。其中，商品零售同比增长 1.0%，比 1-7 月回落 0.1
个百分点，餐饮消费同比增长 2.4%，比 1-7月回落 0.2个百分点。
1-8月，除汽车以外的消费品零售额同比增长 2.7%，与 1-7月
持平。
8 月，社会消费品零售总额同比增长 0.4%，比 7 月回落 0.2
个百分点。其中，商品零售同比增长 0.3%，比 7 月回落 0.2 个百
分点，餐饮消费同比增长 1.1%，比 7月提高 0.3个百分点。
8 月，除汽车以外的消费品零售额同比增长 2.5%，与 7 月持

平。
六、预计9月经济增速小幅提高。
8月，房地产市场拖累有所减弱，PPI环比、同比均有所回升，
投资和消费增速仍然惯性下滑，经济持续回升向好基础还需巩固。
9月，随着宏观政策加力增效，逆周期调节力度增加，预计工业增
加值增速小幅提高。

## Document 207: CTCP Nhựa Thiếu Niên Tiền Phong - Báo cáo cơ hội đầu tư (2026-09-16)

Report source: Unknown

Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

N

BÁO CÁO NGẮN CƠ HỘI ĐẦU TƯ

15/09/2026

CTCP Nhựa Tiền Phong (NTP) 
Tăng tốc trong chu kỳ thuận lợi

Mua 
Giá mục tiêu VND 73.200 
Vùng hỗ trợ/Điểm mua 1 50.000 – 51.400 
Lợi nhuận kỳ vọng 1 44% 
Vùng hỗ trợ/Điểm mua 2 42.000 – 44.000 
Lợi nhuận kỳ vọng 2 70%

Lưu ý: 
Nhà đầu tư cần kết hợp nhận định xu hướng thị 
trường chung, mức độ hấp dẫn của các điểm 
nhấn đầu tư và khẩu vị rủi ro của mình để quản 
lý tỷ trọng tại từng điểm mua cho phù hợp. 
Do việc xác định mỗi điểm mua được dựa trên 
các khung thời gian khác nhau nên chúng tôi 
không đưa ra mức dừng lỗ cố định. Nhìn chung, 
mức dừng lỗ nên được đặt chặt hơn cho điểm 
mua gần (5-7%) và rộng hơn cho điểm mua xa 
(10-15%).

ĐIỂM NHẤN ĐẦU TƯ 
Kỳ vọng sản lượng tiêu thụ tăng trưởng mạnh hơn so với giai đoạn trước. KBSV 
dự báo sản lượng tiêu thụ của NTP tăng 2,3% CAGR giai đoạn 2026 - 2028 nhờ (1) 
Đẩy mạnh giải ngân đầu tư hạ tầng tại miền Bắc (2) gia tăng đầu tư hạ tầng tại các 
địa phương thu hút FDI (3) tăng tốc đơn hàng mảng dự án.

Giá hạt nhựa sẽ gây áp lực biên lợi nhuận nửa cuối 2026 nhưng không làm thay 
đổi triển vọng dài hạn. Mặc dù duy trì chính sách dự phòng nguyên liệu cao và đa 
dạng hóa nhà cung cấp, nhưng NTP vẫn đối mặt với rủi ro thu hẹp lợi nhuận, 
nhưng sẽ không làm giảm đáng kể triển vọng tăng trưởng trung hạn. KBSV cho 
rằng tình trạng dư cung hạt nhựa tại Trung Quốc vẫn sẽ duy trì trong trung hạn và 
xung đột được giải quyết, giảm áp lực biên lợi nhuận toàn ngành.

Catalyst thoái vốn kỳ vọng tái định giá doanh nghiệp. Theo kế hoạch thoái vốn 
Nhà nước, SCIC dự kiến thoái toàn bộ 37,1% cổ phần tại NTP. Chúng tôi đánh giá 
việc hiện thực hóa thương vụ có thể giúp NTP (1) thu hút nhà đầu tư chiến lược 
mới; (2) cộng hưởng mô hình kinh doanh; (3) tái định giá doanh nghiệp.

Hoạt động kinh doanh  Diễn biến doanh thu và lợi nhuận thuần 5 năm gần nhất

NTP là một trong những doanh nghiệp đầu 
ngành ống nhựa tại Việt Nam, với thị phần 
khoảng 31% toàn quốc và 60% tại miền Bắc. 
Hoạt động kinh doanh cốt lõi tập trung vào 
sản xuất và phân phối ống nhựa cùng các sản 
phẩm phụ tùng. Nhờ lợi thế về thương hiệu, 
quy mô và mạng lưới phân phối, NTP duy trì 
vị thế dẫn đầu và nền tảng tăng trưởng ổn 
định qua các năm.

Nguồn: Báo cáo công ty, KB Securities Vietnam

Tương quan mặt bằng giá  Diễn biến P/E của doanh nghiệp

NTP đang được giao dịch với mức P/E khoảng 
8,3 lần thấp hơn trung bình 5 năm của cổ 
phiếu. Với kỳ vọng doanh nghiệp sẽ bước vào 
pha tăng trưởng mạnh trong 3 năm tới, chúng 
tôi cho rằng P/E fw 2026F ở mức 9,5x lần phù 
hợp với mức dự phóng tăng trưởng 11,6% 
CAGR giai đoạn 2026 – 2028.

Nguồn: Bloomberg, KB Securities Vietnam

### Page 2

1. Kỳ vọng sản lượng tiêu thụ tăng trưởng mạnh hơn so với giai đoạn 
trước do nhu cầu tăng cao tại thị trường miền Bắc 
Các dự án hạ tầng đang triển khai 
đồng bộ tại miền Bắc

Miền Bắc đang bước vào một chu kỳ đầu tư hạ tầng mới, đặt nền tảng thuận lợi 
cho hoạt động xây dựng, công nghiệp và đô thị trong trung hạn, hàng loạt dự án 
liên vùng quy mô lớn như Vành đai 4 Vùng Thủ đô, tuyến đường sắt Lào Cai – Hà 
Nội – Hải Phòng và các trục Hà Nội – Hải Phòng – Quảng Ninh, qua đó từng bước 
hoàn thiện mạng lưới giao thông chiến lược và nâng cao năng lực liên kết giữa 
các trung tâm kinh tế lớn tại khu vực. Chúng tôi nhận thấy tiến độ triển khai các 
dự án trên có thể khác biệt so với các dự án hạ tầng trong quá khứ về năng lực 
triển khai nhờ (1) thúc đẩy giải ngân đầu tư công, ưu tiên nguồn lực cho các công 
trình trọng điểm và có tính lan tỏa cao; (2) quyết tâm lớn hơn của Chính phủ 
trong việc xử lý các vướng mắc về thủ tục, giải phóng mặt bằng và phân bổ vốn; 
và (3) yêu cầu cao từ việc nâng cao năng lực vận tải, logistics và kết nối giữa Hà 
Nội với các trung tâm sản xuất, khu công nghiệp và cảng biển phía Bắc.

Khi các tuyến giao thông chiến lược dần hoàn thiện, khả năng tiếp cận các khu 
vực vệ tinh được cải thiện sẽ hỗ trợ khai thác quỹ đất mới và thu hút thêm dòng 
vốn vào bất động sản, khu công nghiệp và logistics. Về mặt dài hạn, việc đầu tư 
hiện tại mang lợi ích kép do (1) gia tăng giá trị xây dựng trực tiếp; (2)  mở rộng 
không gian kinh tế, thúc đẩy đầu tư tư nhân và đặt nền tảng phát triển dài hạn.

Thị phần của NTP Các doanh nghiệp còn lại

### Page 3

3

cung ứng theo chiến lược China +1, chúng tôi cho rằng sức hút FDI của khu vực 
bao gồm (1) hệ sinh thái sản xuất điện tử và công nghệ cao đã hình thành tương 
đối hoàn chỉnh, tạo hiệu ứng thu hút các nhà cung cấp và doanh nghiệp phụ trợ; 
(2) vị trí thuận lợi trong kết nối với chuỗi cung ứng Trung Quốc và các cảng biển 
lớn phía Bắc; và (3) mạng lưới khu công nghiệp, giao thông và logistics ngày 
càng được hoàn thiện, giúp cải thiện khả năng triển khai và mở rộng hoạt động 
sản xuất.

Ngoài ra, dòng vốn mới đang có xu hướng tập trung nhiều hơn vào các lĩnh vực 
có giá trị gia tăng cao như điện tử, bán dẫn và sản xuất công nghệ cao. Chúng tôi 
kỳ vọng sự mở rộng của các cụm sản xuất này sẽ tạo hiệu ứng lan tỏa sang hoạt 
động đầu tư nhà máy, khu công nghiệp, logistics và phát triển đô thị tại các khu 
vực vệ tinh. FDI không chỉ đóng góp trực tiếp vào năng lực sản xuất mà còn tiếp 
tục là một trong những động lực hỗ trợ tăng trưởng đầu tư và xây dựng tại miền 
Bắc.

Biểu đồ 5. FDI đăng ký theo miền  Biểu đồ 6. Sản lượng tiêu thụ của NTP

Nguồn: Fiinpro, KBSV   Nguồn: NTP, KBSV

Kênh dự án được kỳ vọng đóng góp 
đáng kể vào doanh thu giai đoạn 
2026–2028 
 Kênh dự án sẽ là động lực thúc đẩy sản lượng của NTP trong giai đoạn 2026–
2028 nhờ (1) giá trị các dự án đã được cập nhật và dự kiến triển khai trong năm 
2026 đạt gần 1.750 tỷ đồng, tương đương khoảng 24% kế hoạch doanh thu, đóng 
góp đáng kể vào tăng trưởng doanh thu; (2) NTP đang tăng cường nguồn lực cho 
các Ban Phát triển thị trường và duy trì chính sách giá cạnh tranh nhằm nâng tỷ 
lệ tiếp cận, trúng dự án và mở rộng quy mô đơn hàng.

Chúng tôi dự báo sản lượng tiêu thụ của NTP trong 2026/2027 lần lượt đạt 
150/153 nghìn tấn, tương ứng tăng 12%/2% svck, với tỷ lệ sử dụng công suất cao 
nhất có thể đạt khoảng 72%. Với quan điểm tích cực về sự hưởng lợi của doanh 
nghiệp nhờ thúc đẩy hạ tầng, tăng tỷ lệ đô thị hóa và phát triển tại miền Bắc và 
hợp tác với các dự án xây dựng sẽ giúp NTP đạt được mức sản lượng tiêu thụ ở 
nền mới so với giai đoạn trước.

1.  2. Biên lợi nhuận gộp có thể chịu áp lực trong nửa cuối 2026 nhưng 
không làm thay đổi triển vọng dài hạn.  
Đa dạng hóa nguồn nhập khẩu giúp 
hạn chế rủi ro gián đoạn nguyên liệu

thấp, để đảm bảo hoạt động liên tục và duy trì số ngày tồn kho bình quân 
khoảng 96 ngày, chiếm 31% COGS, nhờ vậy NTP luôn có độ trễ so với biến động 
giá thị trường và chi phí thực tế ghi nhận vào giá vốn. Tuy nhiên, áp lực chi phí có 
thể phản ánh trong các quý sau nếu giá nguyên liệu duy trì ở mức cao trong thời 
gian dài.

svck (từ 646 lên 688 USD/tấn), trong khi NTP giữ nguyên mức tăng giá bán kể từ 
đầu năm, thấp hơn mức điều chỉnh của các đối thủ trong ngành.

thị phần. Việc duy trì mức giá cạnh tranh giúp NTP hạn chế áp lực lên hệ thống 
phân phối, giữ chân khách hàng và củng cố thị phần trong giai đoạn chi phí 
nguyên liệu tăng cao. Nhờ đó, sản lượng tiêu thụ 2Q2026 ước tính tăng 17% svck. 
Chúng tôi kỳ vọng biên lợi nhuận sẽ tạo đáy tại Q3/2026 nhờ (1) giá bán niêm yết 
duy trì ở nền cao (2) giá hạt nhựa hạ nhiệt.

Giá nguyên vật liệu tăng cao trong 
trung hạn gây áp lực tới biên lợi 
nhuận  
 Với chính sách duy trì tồn kho nguyên liệu ở mức cao, tương đương khoảng 3–9 
tháng nhập khẩu, NTP có độ trễ nhất định trong việc phản ánh biến động giá 
hạt nhựa vào giá vốn. Trong trường hợp giá nguyên liệu tăng, lượng tồn kho giá 
thấp có thể giúp duy trì biên lợi nhuận trong ngắn hạn; tuy nhiên, nếu mặt 
bằng giá cao kéo dài vượt quá chu kỳ tồn kho, áp lực lên biên lợi nhuận sẽ phụ 
thuộc đáng kể vào khả năng điều chỉnh giá bán của NTP. Việc tăng giá bán có 
thể giúp bù đắp một phần chi phí đầu vào, nhưng mức độ và thời điểm điều 
chỉnh còn phụ thuộc vào diễn biến cạnh tranh và chính sách chiết khấu trên thị 
trường.

động tiêu cực tới nhu cầu tiêu thụ.

## Document 228: FOMC后美股美债齐跌，“新美联储通讯社”：能源与AI投资正改变通胀格局

Source PDF: all report pdf/FOMC后美股美债齐跌，“新美联储通讯社”：能源与AI投资正改变通胀格局.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

沃什在会后新闻发布会上对未来利率路径释放的信息有限，加剧了市场对更⾼利率和更⻓
期通胀压⼒的担忧。 “ 新美联储通讯社 ”Nick Timiraos 撰⽂指出，美联储⾯对的通胀环境已
经发⽣变化，供给侧的能源价格和需求侧的 AI 投资正在同时给通胀带来新的压⼒；近期⻓
端收益率的持续⾛⾼反应了市场对⻓期通胀和政策可信度的担忧。
摘要：
美联储时隔三年多重启加息，并未给市场带来太⼤意外，但主席沃什在会后新闻发布会上对未
来利率路径释放的信息有限，反⽽加剧了投资者对更⾼利率和更⻓期通胀压⼒的担忧，美股与
美债随之承压。
周三，美联储将联邦基⾦利率⽬标区间上调 25 个基点⾄ 3.75% ⾄ 4% ，这是 2023 年以来⾸次加
息。会议前，美股和美债市场整体表现相对平稳；但在沃什召开新闻发布会期间，⻛险资产和
⻓期美债出现明显抛售。
截⾄收盘，道琼斯⼯业平均指数下跌 631.21 点，跌幅 1.21% ，报 51461.90 点；标普 500 指数下跌
33.92 点，跌幅 0.45% ，报 7551.81 点；纳斯达克综合指数微跌 0.01% ，报 25978.42 点。

美债市场的调整则更加直接。两年期美债收益率上涨 6.5 个基点⾄ 4.725% ，创 2024 年 7 ⽉以来最
⾼收盘⽔平； 10 年期美债收益率上涨 0.8 个基点⾄ 5.003% ，升⾄ 19 年⾼位。 30 年期美债收益率
则⼩幅回落 1.7 个基点⾄ 5.346% 。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⽣活家 搜索

### Page 2

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。
“ 新美联储通讯社 ”Timiraos ：能源冲击和 AI 投资正在改变通胀格局
《华尔街⽇报》记者、被市场视为美联储重要 “ 传声筒 ” 的 Nick Timiraos 指出，此次加息反映出美
联储⾯对的通胀环境已经发⽣变化。
过去⼀年，美联储曾通过降息应对劳动⼒市场⾛弱的⻛险，但就业市场并未出现此前担忧的明
显恶化，失业率反⽽从 4.5% 回落⾄ 4.1% 。与此同时，通胀未能继续向 2% 的⽬标靠拢。
更重要的是，能源价格和 AI 投资正在同时给通胀带来新的压⼒。
伊朗战争导致能源价格重新上涨，柴油价格受到炼化瓶颈等因素影响，涨幅尤其明显。前纽约
联储主席 William Dudley 表⽰，柴油价格表现得 “ 就像原油达到每桶 200 美元 ” 。
与此同时， AI 热潮带来的数据中⼼等基础设施投资正在快速增加经济需求。这类投资对利率变化

并不⼗分敏感，因为⼤量资本已经投⼊，预期回报也远⾼于⼩幅利率变化带来的融资成本影
响。
因此，加息⽆法直接增加原油供应，也难以直接阻⽌ AI 基础设施投资，但可以通过压制其他对
利率更加敏感的经济活动，降低整体需求压⼒。
不过， Timiraos 也提到，市场并⾮⼀致认为美联储应该继续加息。⾼盛经济学家认为，近期核⼼
价格增⻓已有所放缓，部分通胀超调可能来⾃⼀次性因素，进⼀步加息的经济依据仍然有限。
⻓端美债已在交易未来的通胀和美联储货币政策可信度
Timiraos 还指出，近期美债⻓端收益率持续⾛⾼，并不能简单归因于美联储加息。
10 年期美债收益率近期已经升⾄ 5% 以上，接近 20 年来最⾼⽔平。投资者将这⼀⾛势归因于 AI 投
资带来的增⻓预期、数据中⼼融资对资本的竞争，以及市场对未来通胀和美联储最终利率⽔平
的重新评估。
这意味着，美债市场关注的已经不仅是本次加息，⽽是美联储最终需要把利率提⾼到多⾼，以
及⾼利率将维持多久。
Timiraos 特别提到，沃什 7 ⽉新闻发布会期间，⻓期美债收益率曾明显上升，此后⼀直没有完全

回落。法国巴黎银⾏⾸席美国经济学家 James Egelhof 认为，这在⼀定程度上反映了市场对美联
储货币政策可信度的重新评估。
此次会议中， 18 名美联储官员有 16 ⼈预计今年⾄少还需加息⼀次，其中 12 ⼈预计再加息⼀次、 4
⼈预计再加息两次。但沃什没有明确说明究竟还需要多少次加息才能让通胀回到 2% 。
因此，周三股债市场的反应更像是⼀次对未来政策路径的重新定价：短端美债收益率反映市场
对进⼀步加息的预期，⻓端收益率则更多反映对⻓期通胀、经济增⻓和政策可信度的担忧，⽽
美股则受到更⾼⽆⻛险利率对估值的压制。

### Page 3

2
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
TODAY’S INSIGHTS
Financial Markets
Markets ended the week mixed. Elevated oil prices continued
to weigh on Asian markets, while a US CPI outcome broadly in
line with expectations, together with a pullback in oil prices,
supported sentiment in Europe and the US. The decline in oil

prices followed reports that Iran and Gulf states were planning
to meet in Oman on Monday to discuss arrangements for
shipping through the Strait, although the meeting has since
been postponed.
Ongoing hostilities between Saudi-backed forces and the
Houthis in Yemen kept tensions elevated over the weekend,
with reports that Houthi forces had advanced towards Red
Sea coastal areas. Separately, a drone attack launched from
Iraq forced the shutdown of Saudi Arabia’s East-West pipeline.

Elsewhere, US leaders of the world’s largest AI platforms
called for a slowdown in the development of the most advanced
AI models, citing technological risks.
• US equities posted their first gain of the week on Friday as an
inflation print broadly in line with expectations and a drop in oil
prices supported sentiment. The S&P 500 rose 0.9%, although
it still ended the week lower overall, while the Dow Jones and
NASDAQ both gained 1.0%. European equities also advanced,

with the Euro Stoxx 50 rising 0.9% and the FTSE 100 up 0.4%.
• Elevated oil prices continued to weigh on Asian markets. The
Nikkei 225 reversed Thursday’s resilience to lead declines,
falling 1.9%, while the KOSPI lost 1.8% and the Hang Seng
declined 0.6%. Locally, the ASX 200 fell 0.9%, recording its
weakest week since March.
• US Treasury yields moved modestly higher after US CPI
accelerated in August, broadly matching expectations, while
the core measure came in slightly above consensus. The

2-year Treasury yield rose 4bps, while the 10-year yield
was unchanged. Futures markets lifted the probability of a
September rate hike to just under 90%, while November and
December moves remain fully priced, taking the implied year-
end fed funds rate to 4.13%.
• Australian government bond yields tracked moves in
Treasuries as elevated oil prices continued to fuel inflation
concerns. Yields climbed to their highest level since 2011,
with the 3-year yield rising 15bps and the 10-year yield

gaining 12bps. Swap markets price the probability of a
September rate hike at just below 80%, while a move in
November remains fully priced, taking the implied year-end
cash rate to 4.74%.
• FX market moves were relatively subdued. The USD
strengthened modestly as markets increased expectations for
further Fed tightening, with the DXY up 0.1%. The AUD rose
0.2% to 0.7165, while the yen gained 0.5% and sterling added
0.1%. The euro slipped 0.1% against the greenback.

• Oil prices pared some recent gains on reports that Iran and
Gulf states were planning discussions around shipping
through the Strait on Friday, although Brent remained up
8.7% over the week. On Friday, Brent fell 2.8% to US$105/
bbl, while WTI declined 2.4% to US$100/bbl. Despite firmer
inflation data, gold rose 0.7%, while copper was unchanged,
and iron ore declined 1.7% as elevated coking coal prices
continued to weigh on steelmaker margins.
International Data

US CPI inflation accelerated in August from 0.1% to 0.4% as
expected, keeping the annual rate at 3.4%. Food prices rose just
0.1%, but energy prices rebounded 2.1%, 16.3%yr. Excluding
food and energy, prices rose 0.3% in the month and 2.4%yr,
0.1ppts lower than July’s 2.5%yr. Within the core detail, goods
inflation remained benign, prices rising 0.1%, 0.7%yr. Core
service prices rose 0.3%, 3.0%yr, with shelter a dominant
contributor given its weight (0.3%, 3.0%yr).

It remains the case that the CPI ex food, energy and shelter
is below target at 1.8% on a six-month annualised basis,
signalling that the current stance of policy is proving effective in
bringing inflation to heal across discretionary items. Regardless,
the FOMC will make the case that the headline measures have
not made enough progress recently and there is therefore need
to tighten policy further. A 25bp hike is expected this week,
and another is likely at the next meeting in October. In our view,

these decisions will be as much about restoring confidence
in the bond market and managing medium-term inflation
expectations as restricting current inflation. But the FOMC will
justify the decision with the near-term inflation narrative.
University of Michigan consumer sentiment fell from
51.7 to 47.8 in the September preliminary reading. Current
conditions edged down in the month (from 51.9 to 50.9), but
it was principally a deterioration in expectations that drove

the headline result (from 51.5 to 45.8). At the current level,
expectations are weaker than they have been at any point
since 1975, except for March 1980. Medium-term inflation
expectations ticked higher in the month from 3.3% to 3.4%,
while the 1-year view jumped from 4.0% to 4.6%.
UK monthly GDP surprised to the upside in July, rising 0.4%
after a 0.3% increase in June. The 3-month average held at 0.4%.
Local Data
A full update to our forecasts has been published in our

September Market Outlook (see here).

## Document 270: Radar Monthly_消費產業_賴偉中_20260914

Source PDF: all report pdf/Radar Monthly_消費產業_賴偉中_20260914.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 25
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

九月 14, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R   1

產業報告  台灣

Radar Monthly 
消費 
非必需品消費前景黯淡  
重要訊息  
本期 Radar Monthly －消費產業，我們提供：(1)全球運動休閒品牌商財報回
顧；(2)中東衝突對消費影響 ；與 (3) 消費產業 8 月營收回顧。  
評論及分析 
全球運動休閒品牌 2Q26 庫存天數創疫後新高，供應鏈訂單不確定將持續至
4Q26- 1Q27 。國際運動休閒品牌及零售商 2Q26 整體庫存天數飆升至 142
天，年增 8%、季增 13%，創下疫後新高並遠高於歷史平均值之 108 天 (圖
1)；其中，儘管多數品牌庫存數量保持低檔，但在原物料價格上揚與美國關
稅推升產品售價之影響下，客流量及商品轉換率普遍轉差。加上美國實質工
資負成長將壓抑消費動能 (圖 2)，多數品牌計畫維持採購緊縮策略，以應對
2H26 銷售競爭加劇。我們預估，產業庫存調整恐需耗時至少兩個季度，這意
味著，台灣成衣及製鞋供應鏈訂單不確定性將持續至 4Q26-1Q27，且稼動率
不足將延緩利潤率復甦前景。 
中東衝突激化，油價攀升使通膨及升息預期升溫並抑制消費。 中東局勢自 7
月美伊破局以來持續激化。除荷姆茲海峽持續有船隻遭攻擊外，伊朗支持之
胡塞武裝與沙烏地阿拉伯及葉門政府軍的衝突也再擴大，布蘭特油價攀升至
100 元以上，較 6 月低點反彈超過 40%。能源價格高漲引發通膨及升息預期
升溫，十年期美國公債殖利率攀升至 4.97% ，較 6 月低點增加約 0.6 個百分
點。凱基認為中東局勢引發的高能源價格，短時間難以解除，對消費衝擊主
要體現在 1.)物價上漲引發通膨，降低實質購買力以及 2.) 能源價格上漲排擠
非必需消費支出。整體來說，我們維持對非必需消費保守看法，消費產業中
建議關注利基型消費，如自行車、隱形眼鏡等 。 
消費產業 8 月營收回顧，運輸類別為最大亮點。 消費產業 8 月營收亮點為運
輸(航空、貨櫃航運、散裝航運) 及隱形眼鏡。其中貨櫃航運、散裝航運營收分
別年增 54%、30%，反映中東衝突外溢效應，包括可用運力限縮、燃油附加
費轉嫁、能源價格高昂使煤炭替代需求增加等。航空營收年增 28%，成長來
自燃油附加費調 漲，以及出境旅遊與 AI 半導體出口暢旺。隱形眼鏡營收年增
29% ，成長來自中國彩片需求熱絡。  
投資建議 
能源價格高漲不利非必需性消費，建議朝向較不受總體變數影響的利基型消
費個股，以及高運價支撐獲利之運輸類股佈局操作，包括巨大 (9921 TT ，
NT$89.7 ， 增加持股)、 美利達(9914 TT，NT$68.7， 增加持股)、視 陽(6782 TT ，
NT$227.0 ， 增加持股)、望 隼(4771 TT，NT$204.0 ， 增加持股)、鈊 象(3293 TT ，
NT$699，增加持股) 、陽明(2609 TT，NT$59.3，增加持股 )、萬海(261 5 TT，
NT$113.0 ，增加持股) 。 
投資風險 
地緣政治衝突擴大、通膨及利率變化、美國關稅政策變化。  
焦點內容  
1. 國際運動休閒品牌 2Q26 整體庫存天
數季增 13%至 142 天，創疫後新高。
台灣供應鏈訂單不確定性將持續至
4Q26~1Q27，且稼動率不足將延緩利
潤率復甦前景。 
2. 中東局勢持續激化 ， 能源價格高漲推升
通膨及升息預期， 不利非必需消費 。 建
議關注利基型消費如自行車、隱形眼
鏡。 
3. 消費類股 8 月營收亮點集中於運輸類股
(貨櫃、散裝、航空 )以及隱形眼鏡，營
收分別年增 54%、30%、28%、29% 。

台灣隱形眼鏡業者 8 月營收成長加速 
1. 台灣隱形眼鏡廠商 8 月合併營收年增
29%，成長動能較前月進一步加速，  主
要受惠產能持續擴充以及客戶訂單需求
強勁。 
2. 隨著矽水膠及多焦點隱形 眼鏡陸續上
市，業者預期營收將延續逐季成長趨勢
至年底，優於過往季節性。  
3. 我們看好台灣隱形眼鏡業者獲利表現具
上修空間，因為營收成長動能轉強，將
帶動產能利用率提升及產品組合優化，
進一步推升毛利率與獲利能力。

暑假旺季，航空股 8 月營收維持高檔 
1. 航空股 8 月營收月增 0%、年增 28%，
年增大幅成長來自燃油附加費調漲、 AI
半導體出口需求暢旺使貨運單位收益增
加。 
2. 長榮航及華航 8 月營收月增分別為
+2.0% 及-1.3% ，維持暑假旺季水準。 
3. 中東衝突持續激化，航空燃油價格已回
升至每桶 180 美元以上，較 6 月低點回
升超過 60%，將壓抑航空業 4Q26 獲利
爆發性。 
1.  
貨櫃航運股 8 月營收續創近兩年新高 
1. 貨櫃航運股 8 月營收月增 3%、年增
54%， 美國商品補庫存運輸需求強勁 ， 。 
2. 8 月 SCFI 綜合指數月增 7%至 3388 點，
預期貨櫃航運股 9 月營收將繼續保持月
增成長，且營收連續 6 個月成長將創
2021 年以來最佳紀錄。 
3. SCFI 綜合指數 、 歐線 、 美西線 、 美東線、
近洋線， 7/31 至今累計漲幅分別為
14% 、-21%、18%、16%、61%。漲
幅集中於美線及近洋 線，為萬海主要航
線，預期獲利上修空間較長榮及陽明更
大。

美股焦點

Gap ( 美) 主力品牌面臨銷售壓力，庫存天數飆升  
Gap ( 美) 2Q FY26 (5 -7 月；財年止於 1 月) 營收年減 2%至 36.5 億美元，低
於指引之年減 0-1%， 主因主力品牌Old Navy 女裝季節性商品面臨銷售壓力 ，
且客流量意外放緩，導致同店銷售下滑，抵銷 Gap 品牌銷售延續 9%成長。
再加上地緣政治動盪推升在途存貨數量，2 Q FY26 庫存天數飆高至 115 天，
年增 27%、季增 23%，遠高於過去十年同期平均之 91 天。公司調降 FY26
營收展望，並已採取針對性應對措施，力圖重振 Old Navy 產品需求。

Lululemon ( 加) 庫存天數大增，且銷售展望惡化  
Lululemon (加 ) 2Q FY26 ( 5-7 月；財年止於 1 月) 營收年減 4%至 24.2 億美
元，低於公司展望之 2-3%衰退，主因社群媒體負面評論影響客流量，也導致
部分新產品上市後反應不如預期。2Q FY26 庫存天數再度攀升至 160 天，年
增 11%、季增 19%，遠高於過去十年同期平均水準之 131 天。儘管公司已
調整產品組合，但至目前新品發布反響仍不穩定，主力市場銷售持續有壓。
管理層預計 3Q FY26 營收減幅將進一步惡化至 10-11%。

圖 24：摘要   圖 25：Adidas 服飾業務為亮點所在

35%、19%。歐非中東營收年增 7%，反映促銷活動頻繁。

速度。2Q26 庫存天數位處高檔水準。

零售

圖 51：零售產業摘要  圖 52：7 月台灣零售銷售維持穩健成長 
1. 台灣 7 月零售銷售年增 7.7%，維持穩健成長動能，主要受惠車市
買氣回溫、暑假娛樂與 購物需求，以及多元行銷活動挹注；其中
百貨公司表現最佳，年增 9.6%，超級市場與便利商店則分別年增 
6.0% 與 4.2%。 
2. 7 月餐飲業銷售年增僅 2.5%，成長幅度較 6 月的 4.6%放緩，主
要因颱風假停班停課影響消費。 
3. 中國消費持續成長，餐飲收入七月年增率由 1.2% 升至 1.4%，惟
零售銷售增速放緩，年增率由 1.0% 降至 0.6%。

觀光

圖 59：觀光產業摘要  圖 60：台灣入境旅客減少 
1. 台灣飯店業者 8 月合併營收年增 7%，對比過去 10 年平均成長幅
度為 5%，主要受惠季節性餐飲銷售表現穩健，以及休閒旅遊需
求持續增溫。 
2. 台灣 7 月入境旅客人數年增 4.5%。主要區域中，歐洲旅客年增
25.7%，成長最快；美洲及亞洲旅客分別年增 6.0% 及 3.4%，大
洋洲旅客則年減 6.8%。 
3. 隨觀光需求逐步復甦，台灣飯店 2Q26 平均房價年增 3.8%，延
續成長趨勢；住房率則年增 0.5 個百分點，維持穩定。

海運

圖 91 : 航運產業摘要    圖 92：亞洲颱風侵襲頻繁，準班率下降  
1. NRF 預估 8-9 月美國進口貨櫃量均保持 7 月水準 ， 商品補庫存需求
強勁，美線運價達到 2022 年以來新高。 
2. Maersk(丹)、Hapeg-Lloyd(德)、CMA CGM(法)等頭部航商陸續重返
試航紅海航道，蘇伊士運河通行量小幅回升。 
3. 亞洲颱風活動活躍，上海及寧波港 8 月以來數度中斷營運，積壓
大量運力造成嚴重塞港，並推升運價上漲。Linerlytica 統計全球錨
地運力超過 500 萬 TEU，佔全球總運力達 14.6%。 
4. 9 月起進入北美穀物出口旺季，疊加巴拿馬運河降載衝擊 ，散裝運
價攀升至近四年高位。

航空

圖 99：航空產業摘要    圖 100：航空燃油價格較 6 月低點反彈超過 50% 
1. 客 運，9 月暑假結束進入小月，不過仍有中秋連假支撐出境旅遊，
預期 9 月客運營收將月減 15~20%。 
2. 貨運，AI SERVER及半導體出貨需求持續放大，同時9 月起進入傳
統空運旺季，貨運單位收益及營收可望逐月成長。 
3. 桃園機場 7 月處理貨量年增率由 12.3%大幅下降至 3.7%，主要
是轉口貨量較少所致，出口貨量仍維持雙位數年增成長。 
4. 中東衝突持續激化，航空燃油價格已回升至每桶 180 美元以上，
較 6 月低點回升超過 60%，將壓抑航空業 4Q26 獲利爆發性。

汽車

圖 107：汽車產業摘要   圖 108：台灣 8 月汽車銷量呈現衰退 
1. 台灣 8 月汽車銷量年減 1%至 2.9 萬輛 (對比過去 10 年平均 3.1 萬
輛)，主要受到國產車廠歲修影響，其中國產車年減 9%、進口車
年增 8%。 
2. 累計 1-8 月台灣汽車銷量為 27 萬輛，年增 2.4，和泰車 (2207 TT, 
NT$538, 未評等) 下修展望自先前的 44 萬輛至 43 萬輛，但維持集
團銷售目標 16.5 萬不變，隱含市佔率將提升至 38.4% 
3. Tesla (美) 8 月銷量達 1,737 輛，占整體電動車銷量 53%，其中
Model Y 與 Model 3 分別達 788 輛、949 輛，帶動整體電動車銷量
達 3,259 輛，電動車滲透率攀升至 11.2%。

## Document 273: Restoring the RBNZ’s dual mandate_ mechanics and possible implications (2026-09-17)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Restoring the RBNZ’s dual mandate_ mechanics and possible implications (2026-09-17).pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

1
Westpac New Zealand Economics
Restoring the RBNZ’s dual mandate: mechanics and
possible implications
• The next general election will take place on
7 November. Current polling is very tight and thus
clients are understandably interested in how
economic policy settings might evolve should there
be a change in Government.
• If elected to lead the next Government, the
Labour Party has pledged to restore the RBNZ's
dual mandate, requiring monetary policy to

achieve price stability while supporting maximum
sustainable employment.
• Reinstating the dual mandate would require
amendments to the Reserve Bank of New Zealand
Act 2021 and the issuance of a new Remit by the
Minister of Finance.
• The legislative process could be completed
relatively quickly, with the National Party-led
Government demonstrating in 2023 that significant
mandate changes can be enacted within weeks.
• For demand-driven economic cycles, a dual

mandate would likely have little impact on OCR
decisions, as inflation and employment objectives
generally move in the same direction.
• The main difference would emerge during supply
shocks, such as that occurring at present, where
inflation rises even as economic activity and
employment are weakened, forcing policymakers
to balance competing objectives.
• We believe a restored dual mandate could result
in a slightly slower return of inflation to target,

a slower rise in or slightly lower peak OCR and
slightly greater tolerance of temporary inflation
overshoots when labour market conditions
are weak.
One of the more significant economic policy proposals
heading into the General Election on 7 November is
the Labour Party’s proposal to restore the RBNZ’s dual
inflation and employment mandate. The proposal would
reverse one of the first legislative changes made by the
National Party-led Government after taking office in

late 2023, when it removed the objective of supporting
maximum sustainable employment and returned
the RBNZ to the prior single statutory objective of
price stability.
Current polling is very tight and thus clients are
understandably interested in how economic policy
settings might evolve should there be a change in
Government. In this article we discuss what would need
to be done to give effect to the Labour Party’s proposed
policy change, together with the more important question

of whether a return to dual objectives would materially
alter future monetary policy decisions.
What is the Labour Party proposing?
The Labour Party has indicated it would restore the
monetary policy framework that existed between 2018
and 2023. Under that framework, the RBNZ's Monetary
Policy Committee (MPC) was required to formulate policy
in a manner consistent with two economic objectives:
• achieving and maintaining stability in the general level

of prices; and
• supporting maximum sustainable employment.
It is worth emphasising that the concept of “maximum
sustainable employment” is distinct from “maximum
employment”. That is, it is recognised that there
will always be some level of unemployment or
underutilisation in a dynamic economy, and that the level
of unemployment consistent with stable inflation will also
16 Sep 2026 | Westpac NZ Economics Team | westpac.co.nz/economics | economics@westpac.co.nz

ECONOMIC BULLETIN

### Page 2

2
Westpac New Zealand Economics
depend on frictional and structural factors impacting the
labour market.
The dual mandate was introduced in 2018 through
amendments to the former Reserve Bank Act and
subsequently carried over into the Reserve Bank of New
Zealand Act 2021. In December 2023, the National Party-
led Government removed the employment objective,
restoring a sole focus on price stability. Therefore, a
future Labour Party-led Government would largely be

reinstating a framework that was already in place prior to
the current Government's reforms.
At this stage, it is not clear whether there would be any
tweaks to the prior framework, such as seeking to express
the employment goal as a numerical target. However,
we think that both a future Government and the RBNZ
would be reluctant to go down this route. There is a high
degree of uncertainty about what constitutes “maximum
sustainable employment”, and conceptually this can

change over time depending on other factors (including
other economic policies) impacting the operation of the
labour market. For this reason, the RBNZ has historically
emphasized that the concept of “maximum sustainable
employment” is difficult to capture in a single indicator,
and there has been no presumption that the RBNZ can
influence factors such as the equilibrium employment
rate or labour force participation rate.
What legislative changes would be required?

Restoring the dual mandate would require both legislative
change and a new Remit to provide operational guidance
to the MPC.
The primary step would be amending the Reserve
Bank of New Zealand Act 2021. The 2023 legislation
removed the employment objective from the Act and
established price stability as the sole economic objective
of monetary policy. A future Labour Government
would need to introduce an amendment bill reversing
those changes and reinstating "supporting maximum

sustainable employment" as a statutory objective.
Because the previous framework already existed, the
legislative drafting exercise would likely be relatively
straightforward. In practical terms, Parliament could
simply restore much of the wording that existed prior to
the 2023 amendment.
Following the removal of the dual mandate in 2023, the
Remit was amended to remove references to maximum
sustainable employment while retaining the inflation
objective of keeping CPI inflation between 1% and 3%,

with a focus on the 2% midpoint over the medium term. A
Labour Government would need to issue a revised Remit
alongside the legislative amendments.
The revised Remit would likely retain the existing inflation
target band; restore references to maximum sustainable
employment; require the MPC to assess labour market
conditions alongside inflation outcomes; and explain
how policy decisions balance the two objectives. It
is worth noting that the RBNZ already monitors and

reports on many labour market variables (see, for
example, the “heatmap” depicted in Figure 2.9 of the
September 2026 Monetary Policy Statement). Under
the current framework, contributing to its assessment
of how the overall economy is tracking relative to its
productive potential.
How quickly could these changes occur?
Once a new Government is formed, experience suggests
the process could move rapidly. The National-led
Government was officially sworn in on 27 November

2023. A week later Cabinet agreed to remove the RBNZ’s
dual mandate. An amendment bill was introduced on
12 December 2023, the same day that a revised Remit
was issued by the Minister of Finance. This bill was
passed under urgency and received Royal Assent on 19
December 2023 before coming into force (along with the
Remit) a day later. Thus, the whole process was complete
just over three weeks after the new Government
took office.
A future Labour Party-led Government could potentially

move at a similar pace if it regarded restoration of the
dual mandate as a priority (whether it would be a priority
is unclear at this stage). Achieving fully legislated change
before the Christmas holiday break will be much more
difficult than in 2023, given that this year’s election is
three weeks later than that which took place in 2023, and
so official results will not be released until 27 November.
It is also unclear how long it would take to form a new

Labour Party-led Government. However, by law, the last
day for the new Parliament to sit is 14 January 2027 – six
weeks after the return of the writ on 3 December. So,
while unlikely, if Parliament was to sit in mid-January, the
new arrangements could theoretically be in place ahead
of the MPC’s first meeting next year, which is scheduled to
take place on 10 February.
Given the timeframes above, the MPC meeting on 9
December this year will almost certainly take place under

the current legislation and Remit. That said, knowledge
of impending changes could conceivably colour an
MPC member’s view at that meeting if the change was
already progressing, or about to progress, through the
legislative process.
Would a dual mandate produce different
interest rate outcomes?
The practical impact of restoring the dual mandate
depends heavily on the type of economic shock
confronting policymakers.
Historically, cycles in the New Zealand economy have

been dominated by demand shocks. In the case of
demand-driven cycles, a dual mandate will likely produce

### Page 3

3
Westpac New Zealand Economics
little change in monetary policy decisions. This is
because when demand is running too strongly, inflation
tends to rise and the labour market tends to tighten
simultaneously. In this situation, both objectives point
in the same direction. A rise in the OCR will act to both
lower inflation and ease pressure in the labour market.
Conversely, when demand weakens, inflation pressures
fall and unemployment rises. A decline in the OCR will act

to both prevent an undue decline in inflation and a rise
in unemployment.
In these circumstances, price stability and maximum
sustainable employment are largely complementary
objectives rather than competing ones. This is one reason
why monetary policy outcomes under the dual-mandate
framework between 2018 and 2023 were not obviously
different from what might have occurred under a single
mandate. Indeed, the RBNZ delivered one of the most
aggressive tightening cycles in its history between 2021

and 2023 as inflation surged (the latter, initially due
to the supply shock that accompanied the pandemic,
but later in response to the aggressive monetary and
fiscal policy easing that occurred in New Zealand and
most elsewhere). In these circumstances, employment
considerations did not prevent substantial OCR increases
because both high inflation and historically tight labour
market indicators pointed toward the need for tighter
monetary policy.

The biggest practical differences arise during supply
shocks, such as the spike in global energy prices (and
related products) that we are presently seeing because
of the Middle East conflict (major trade disruptions,
climate-related events and pandemics fall into the
same category). Supply shocks often raise inflation
while simultaneously reducing economic activity and
employment. Under a single inflation mandate, the
MPC's primary focus is restoring price stability. While

policymakers would still consider broader economic
conditions – including the longstanding secondary
objective of avoiding unnecessary volatility in the real
economy and financial conditions – the statutory
objective clearly prioritises inflation control. In the
past the RBNZ has rightly emphasised that there is
no long run trade-off between the labour market and
inflation. Although there may be scope, depending on
circumstances, for some shorter run trade-offs that could

be exploited that see inflation deviating from target for a
bit longer to smooth the impact on the labour market.
Under a dual mandate, however, policymakers would
be formally required to consider the employment
consequences of their actions alongside the inflation
outlook. They would need to seek balance in
recommending policy actions that whilst helping to
achieve one objective, might be detrimental to achieving
the other. In practice, this could mean greater tolerance

of temporary inflation overshoots; a slower pace of policy
tightening; a longer horizon for returning inflation to
target; and therefore, a somewhat lower peak in the OCR
than would otherwise be the case.
What would it mean for the OCR outlook in
the current context?
In normal economic conditions, the practical implications
are likely to be very modest. Central banks, including
the RBNZ, recognise that low and stable inflation is a
prerequisite for sustained employment growth over

the longer term. Consequently, most monetary policy
decisions do not involve sharp trade-offs between
inflation and employment objectives and there is
certainly no trade-off in the long run.
But in the current context, with the unemployment rate
at an 11-year high, an explicit dual mandate would likely
encourage the MPC to place greater weight on potential
employment outcomes than would be the case under
the current single mandate. This might lead the MPC to

tolerate a slightly longer horizon for returning inflation to
target, leading to a slightly lower peak in the OCR than
would otherwise be the case (but a rate that would likely
remain elevated for longer given the slower adjustment to
the inflation shock).
However, in practice things may not be that clear. For
short-lived supply shocks, there’s a clear case for looking
through the inflation and reducing pressure on the
labour market. However, recently we have been in an

environment of more frequent supply shocks. If inflation
expectations are high or rising the RBNZ might still
conclude that some labour market slack is required to
ensure inflation returns to target – even over an elongated
timeframe. In the current case, it could be the case that
the MPC might be encouraged to merely slow the pace
at which they reduce stimulus/introduce restrictive
conditions if they are uncertain about how quickly the
labour market will return to a necessarily poorly defined

estimate of “maximum sustainable employment”.
The views recently expressed by clients seem to reflect
the idea that the peak OCR could be reduced under a
dual mandate in the current circumstances. In our recent
RBNZ pulse survey, half of respondents thought that
the reintroduction of the ‘dual mandate’ would lead
to a moderately (25-50bps) lower peak in the OCR for
the current cycle. And an informal poll during one of
our recent client functions found mixed views on what

a change of Government might mean for economic
activity next year, but around half thought that the main
impact would be higher inflation than otherwise. We
should remember that a new government might lead
to multiple shifts in policy settings, for example higher
levels of government spending or higher future minimum
wage settings.

### Page 4

4
Westpac New Zealand Economics
Finally, it is worth noting that at this stage election
polling remains tight. And it is unclear to what extent
current market pricing for the RBNZ’s policy settings in
2027 might already incorporate some allowance for the
possibility that there will be a change in Government and
the restoration of the dual mandate.

## Document 304: US MARKET CALL_ Still Targeting 8400, But Raising Odds Of Bearish Outcome To 30%

Source PDF: all report pdf/US MARKET CALL_ Still Targeting 8400, But Raising Odds Of Bearish Outcome To 30%.pdf
Report source: Substack Reports
Date: 2026-09-13
Pages: 11
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

From:    Yardeni QuickTakes <yardeni-research@ghost.io>
Subject: US MARKET CALL: Still Targeting 8400, But Raising Odds Of Bearish Outcome To 30%
Date:    4 hours ago

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
US MARKET CALL: Still
Targeting 8400, But Raising
Odds Of Bearish Outcome To
30%
By Ed Yardeni & Toby Hearst • 12 Sept 2026
View in browser

### Page 2

Photo by Ben Saxon / Unsplash
I. Performance
The good news is that our recommendation to overweight the S&P 500
Energy sector is working well (chart). The bad news is that it is doing so
because the war in the Middle East has re-escalated. Saudi Arabia has
shut down its East-West pipeline as a precaution after a drone attack that
originated in Iraq. The pipeline had been moving 4 million to 5 million
barrels of oil per day, amounting to 4% to 5% of global supply.

Oil prices shot up over the past week, and the retail price of diesel in the
United States surged past a record-high $6 a gallon. Jacking the prices

### Page 3

were two developments: The US and Iran both fired on oil tankers, and
the Iran-aligned Houthis in Yemen advanced along the Red Sea,
potentially extending disruption to another major oil route. If the Houthis
close the Bab el-Mandeb Strait, that would deal another economic blow,
constraining another 7% of global petroleum supplies and about 12% of
global trade.
The latest rebound in oil prices above $100 a barrel boosted the 10-year
US Treasury bond yield to nearly 5.00% and the comparable TIPS yield to

2.60% (chart). Yields are rising on fears that the war in the Middle East is
far from over, resulting in higher-for-longer oil prices. If so, then the latest
oil shock could spread inflationary pressures in the economy. That would
increase the odds of a prolonged Fed rate-hiking cycle.

### Page 4

Yet the S&P 500 is down just 1.8% from its record high of 7798.99 on
August 13 (chart). Over that same period, the equal-weight version of the
index is down 3.5%.
II. Earnings
FEMO (Fabulous Earnings Momentum) continues to support stock prices
despite the latest troubling developments. The S&P 500 forward earnings
has risen to a record $403.44 per share (chart). By definition, it is
approaching the analysts' consensus 2027 EPS estimate and will
converge with that estimate by year-end. The consensus has risen to

$419.54, and we think it could rise further to $425 by year-end. By the
way, the 2027 estimate is not distorted by mark-to-market (MTM) capital
gains, as is the 2026 estimate.

### Page 5

S&P 500 forward earnings has been rising at a faster pace in recent
weeks (chart). This suggests that the underlying trend in actual earnings
(excluding the recent MTM gains) remains very strongly to the upside.
FEMO can be explained by the surprisingly strong pace of S&P 500
revenue growth (chart). It has accelerated this year, notwithstanding rising
oil prices and bond yields.
FEMO has also gotten a big boost from a spike in the S&P 500 profit
margin (chart). The S&P 500 forward profit margin rose to a record-high

16.8% in early September, confirming that the trend is solidly to the upside
even excluding the MTM distortion during the first two quarters of this
year.

### Page 6

On a y/y basis, there is no sign of any slowdowns in the growth rates of
either S&P 500 forward revenues or S&P 500 forward earnings (chart).
Even more remarkable is that analysts' consensus long-term earnings
growth (LTEG) expectation continues to rise to record highs (chart). Last
week, LTEG rose to 26.7%. Of course, such a rate of growth would be
impossible given that nominal GDP growth is well below that. However, it
reflects analysts' collective exuberance about the earnings prospects of

the companies they follow.

### Page 7

The dispersion of positive y/y percentage changes in S&P 500 forward
revenues and forward earnings remains very high (chart).
The S&P 500 Net Earnings Revisions Index also rose to a cyclical high in
September (chart).

### Page 8

The forward earnings of the S&P 500, S&P 400, and S&P 600 all rose to
record highs last week (chart).
III. Valuation
Since the start of the year, the S&P 500’s forward earnings has risen
28.1%, while its forward P/E has fallen 12.9% ytd (chart). Investors aren’t
willing to pay as much for FEMO as they were in January. They may be
concerned that analysts' EPS projections reflect a fair amount of irrational
exuberance, even though that exuberance has been fueled by actual EPS

results!

### Page 9

In recent weeks, the forward P/Es of the major market indexes have
declined as forward EPS estimates have outpaced stock price gains
(chart). FEMO has been partially offset by less FOMO!
Also weighing on forward P/Es has been the rise in bond yields (chart).
We are still projecting 8400 for the S&P 500 by the end of the year. That
target might be hit with stronger forward earnings and a weaker forward
P/E than we had expected. So we are raising our 2027 earnings estimate

to $425 per share from $415 and lowering our forward P/E expectation to
19.7 from 20.2.

### Page 10

IV. Sentiment
Admittedly, recent developments in the oil and bond markets are
unnerving. We are acknowledging that by lowering the odds of our
Roaring 2020s scenario through the end of the decade from 80% to 70%.
We are raising the odds of a more bearish outcome from 20% to 30%.
September has often been the toughest month of the year for stock
investors. That could be the case this year too. The bull-bear ratios we
track could fall sharply over the rest of the month because of the concerns

we discussed above (chart). From a contrarian perspective, we would
welcome that as a strong buying signal.

contributions may be featured in our segment, "Ed Answers
Your Questions".

## Document 325: 【國泰證期研究部】4Q26 AI產業展望-AI驅動SaaS開啟第二條成長曲線-20260917

Source PDF: all report pdf/【國泰證期研究部】4Q26 AI產業展望-AI驅動SaaS開啟第二條成長曲線-20260917.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

1

4Q26 AI 產業展望 
AI 驅動 SaaS 開啟第二條成長曲線

### Page 2

2 
4Q26 AI 產業展望 
AI 驅動 SaaS 開啟第二條成長曲線 
結論與建議 
1. 4Q26 AI Agent 促進 SaaS 新一輪競賽，介面比競爭對手好用這個優勢，在 AI Agent 時代
將會是極高的風險，並且過往在利基型應用上，企業通常因為自己做成本太高，而選擇買
SaaS 產品，Agentic Coding 降低了製作的成本，功能單一且缺乏專有資料的產品亦將被
淘汰。但企業重要資料、工作流程等才具有重要戰略價值。 
2. Outcome-based Pricing 將發生作用，SaaS 產品的價格天花板從 software budget 變成
labor economics，願意支付價格=被替代或增加之工作的經濟價值，低價值 SaaS TAM
將縮小，反之高價值 Agentic Software TAM 擴大。 
3. 未來 SaaS KPI 觀察重點也將逐步轉變為 Agent ARR/ Consumption，以及確認 AI 是否提
高 existing customer expansion(NRR) ，另外因 Agent 將帶來額外的 token 以及推論成
本，AI Gross Margin 亦將用來確認收入品質。

4Q26 投資展望 
1. MSFT 在 Agent 時代勝率亦最高，MSFT 幾乎每一層都有產品，FY4Q26 MSFT Azure 年
度收入首次突破 1,000 億美元 ， 而M365 Copilot付費席次已超過 3,000 萬 。 另外Snowflake 
將企業分散在不同系統和雲端的零散數據集中管理，讓跨部門快速查詢與分析，目前RPO
年增 30%，NRR 達 126%，AI Agent 大規模進入企業，每個Agent 背後都需要 context、
structured data 這些全部會提高 data consumption。 
2. Agent 最後一定要執行 workflow execution 這些正是 ServiceNow 的核心，目前RPO 年
增 21%，AI ACV(年度合約價值)突破 10 億美元 ，agentic deployments 九個月 9 倍成長 。
Datadog 則是典型賣鏟子標的，只要Agent 數量增加，production environment 的複雜
度就會上升 ，Datadog 是直接的受惠者 。Palantir 則證明當 AI 軟體真正可以進入企業核心
營運 、 供應鏈 、 決策與工作流程時 ， 企業願意支付的金額可以遠大於傳統模式 ， 也是Agentic 
SaaS 最終的產品形態。

### Page 3

3 
4Q26 AI Agent 促進 SaaS 新一輪競賽 
過去最典型的 SaaS 商業模式非常簡單，約可以簡化為 SaaS Revenue=使用者數×每席價格×滲
透率，企業員工增加，軟體 seat 自然增加，軟體公司再透過新增模組、升級 premium plan 與
cross-sell 提高 ARPU(每用戶平均收入)。 
過去最好的 SaaS 公司都有三個共同特徵：企業員工數長期成長、seat 數增加，以及 NRR(淨收
入留存率)高於 100%。但 AI Agent 改變了這個公式，例如未來企業不是讓 500 名員工各自登
入軟體介面，而是讓 100 名員工加上數百個 AI Agent，故重點轉向為新增的 Agent Revenue
能不能大於流失的 Human Seat Revenue。

圖 1. SaaS business model

資料來源：國泰證期研究部整理

過去最傳統企業軟體最大的價值之一，是提供操作介面。例如員工進入 CRM 查詢客戶、進
ERP 查庫存、進 HR 系統請假、進 ITSM 系統開 ticket。但 Agent 出現後，使用者不一定需要
知道底層是哪套軟體，例如員工只要告訴企業 Agent 需要產出何種結果即可，Agent 可以自行
呼叫 CRM、ERP、客服平台、Email、文件系統與資料倉儲，故 Interface Layer 的價值下降，
Data、Workflow、Execution Layer 的價值上升。簡而言之，介面比競爭對手好用這個優勢，
在 AI Agent 時代將會是極高的風險，並且過往利基型應用企業通常因為自己做成本太高，而選
擇買 SaaS 產品，Agentic Coding 降低了製作的成本，功能單一且缺乏專有資料的產品亦將被
淘汰。但企業重要資料、工作流程等才具有重要戰略價值。

### Page 4

4 
大型 SaaS 反而迎來第二次成長 
我們預估 Outcome-based Pricing 將發生作用，也就是過去 SaaS 公司主要競爭的是企業 IT 預
算，但 AI Agent 在可以完成工作下，企業評估 ROI 的方法會改變，例如一套客服 SaaS 每年
100 萬美元，以前 CFO 會比較另一家軟體是不是只要 80 萬美元？，但未來考量重點將在於這
100 萬美元的 Agent 能不能減少 500 萬美元的人工作業成本？定價基準就完全不同。 
SaaS 產品的價格天花板從 software budget 變成 labor economics。 
也就是願意支付價格=被替代或增加之工作的經濟價值。這也是為什麼 Agent 同時造成兩個看
似矛盾的結果：低價值 SaaS TAM 縮小，高價值 Agentic Software TAM 擴大。 
假設企業原本支付 500 萬美元給軟體、5,000 萬美元支付人工。如果 Agent 讓軟體費用從 500
萬提高到 1,000 萬，但人工與外包成本降低 2,000 萬，企業整體 ROI 仍然大幅改善。

圖 2. Outcome-based Pricing

資料來源：Paid.ai、國泰證期研究部整理

另外如同前述，未來企業軟體競爭中資料為重中之重，Agent 本身並不擁有企業真實世界資
訊，AI 模型雖然知道什麼是客戶，但只有 CRM 知道客戶買了什麼，模型知道什麼是員工，但
只有 Workday 知道員工的職位、薪資與權限，模型知道什麼是 server，但只有 ServiceNow、
Datadog 或企業 CMDB 知道這台 server 目前出什麼問題。 
未來 SaaS KPI 觀察重點也將逐步轉變為 Agent ARR/ Consumption，以及確認 AI 是否提高
existing customer expansion(NRR)，另外因 Agent 將帶來額外的 token 以及推論成本，AI 
Gross Margin 亦將用來確認收入品質。

### Page 5

5 
SaaS 勝出關鍵在於 Data 以及 Workflow 
SaaS 龍頭 MSFT 仍將是主要受惠廠商，在 Agent 時代勝率亦最高，主因同時擁有多項產品例如
Microsoft 365、Outlook、Azure…等，Agent 真正要完成工作，需要同時知道看什麼、資料在
哪、要用哪個 tool、誰批准、如何執行，MSFT 幾乎每一層都有產品，FY4Q26 MSFT Azure 年
度收入首次突破 1,000 億美元，而 M365 Copilot 付費席次已超過 3,000 萬。 
另外 Snowflake 將企業分散在不同系統和雲端的零散數據集中管理 ， 讓跨部門快速查詢與分析，
目前 RPO 年增 30%，NRR 達 126%，AI Agent大規模進入企業 ， 每個Agent 背後都需要 context、
structured data 這些全部會提高 data consumption。 
ServiceNow 它將企業內部繁雜的跨部門事務 （如資訊 、 人資 、 客服） 整合在單一的雲端平台 Now 
Platform 上運行，透過自動化規則與工作流程，讓事件從偵測、分派到解決都能直接在平台上
完成，Agent 最後一定要執行 workflow execution 這些正是 ServiceNow 的核心， 目前RPO 年
增 21%，AI ACV(年度合約價值)突破 10 億美元，agentic deployments 九個月 9 倍成長。 
Datadog 則是典型賣鏟子標的，簡單來說 ， 它就像是企業 IT 系統的全天候健檢中心與保全系統 ，
能將伺服器 、 資料庫 、 應用程式 、 網路設備以及雲端服務的數據集中在單一儀表板上，只要 Agent
數量增加，production environment 的複雜度就會上升，AI application、agent、inference 與
security workload 持續爆炸，Datadog 是直接的受惠者。 
Palantir 則證明當 AI 軟體真正可以進入企業核心營運、供應鏈、決策與工作流程時，企業願意支
付的金額可以遠大於傳統模式，Palantir 並沒有把 AI 賣成聊天機器人，而是 Data + Workflow 
+ AI 綁在一起，也是 Agentic SaaS 最終的產品形態。

### Page 6

6

### Page 7

## Document 420: 債券市場總評：通訊債攻守兼備

Source PDF: all report pdf/債券市場總評：通訊債攻守兼備.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

1

台北富邦銀行 
Sep 4，2026 | 3:00 PM (GMT+8) 
債券市場總評： 通訊債 攻守兼備
投資研究部 
 Research 
Financial Market 
▎通膨疑慮未退，華許維持偏鷹 
8 月下旬以來，美債殖利率維持高檔震盪偏上行。美伊
衝突再度升溫、雙方強硬放話延續，使油價維持高檔波
動，通膨疑慮仍未完全消退。7 月 PCE 與核心 PCE 仍
高於 Fed 2%目標，加上聯準會主席華許於全球央行年
會延續偏鷹基調 ， 強調需確認通膨明確且快速朝目標回
落 ，否則仍可能採取行動，使市場一度上修9 月升息預
期 。 不過 ， 近日 ADP 與 JOLTs 就業數據降溫 ， 部分Fed
官員釋出偏鴿訊息 ， 帶動殖利率自高點小幅回落 ， 但10
年期美債殖利率仍維持在 4.7%上方，市場轉向觀望非
農就業數據。 
圖 1 PCE 仍高於目標，勞動市場降溫

資料來源：Bloomberg，2026/9/3 
▎油價牽動殖利率震盪，債市承壓 
殖利率高檔震盪仍是近期債市的主要壓力。雖然先前
油價回落與經濟信心轉弱，曾帶動殖利率自高位下
滑，主要債券同步反彈。不過，油價與殖利率再度走
高後，美國公債與投等債漲幅回吐，非投等債則受風
險偏好支撐，相對抗跌。近期殖利率雖自高點小幅回
落，帶動債市跌幅收斂，但美國投等債 YTD 報酬仍下
跌 0.59%。

圖 2 債市反彈後回吐，修復力道有限

資料來源：Bloomberg，2026/9/3

◼ 中東局勢升溫推高油價，加上 PCE 仍高於 Fed 目標，通膨疑慮未退，華許偏鷹表
態使升息預期再度波動。就業數據雖降溫，但美債殖利率仍處高檔，壓抑債 市，
不過近期通訊債報酬表現領先主要產業，利差也轉趨收斂，呈現相對修復跡象。 
◼ 通訊債基本面仍具支撐，大型電信商用戶基礎穩定，寬頻業務成長有助支撐營收
與現金流能見度。主要電信商今年自由現金流可望成長 6.7%，寬頻用戶預估年增
約 7%~9%，有助支撐中期現金流表現，整體槓桿可控，付息能力仍維持穩定。 
◼ 在相對估值上，通訊債仍偏便宜，利差補償 較高，投資吸引力仍在。大型電信債
的優勢，主要來自用戶基礎穩定、現金流轉化較佳，且債務負擔仍可控，配置上
可偏向現金流與信用條件較佳的發行公司。媒體娛樂發行公司則可聚焦高評等、
低槓桿或自由現金流較佳者 。 若擔憂短線殖利率波動風險， 可偏向中短天期債券 。

美國投等債報酬率 -0.59 
美國公債報酬率 -0.59

2

誠信親切、專業創新 週報 
▎通訊債利差收斂，殖利率水準居前 
今年債市表現承壓，除了油價上漲、通膨黏性與長天期
利率高檔震盪外 ，AI 與資料中心投資帶動大型企業發債
增加，也使投資級債市面臨更多供給壓力。科技與通訊
產業債受影響較明顯，其中通訊債又受到 Meta*、
AT&T*、Verizon*等大型發行人供給增加影響，7 月利
差一度被推寬。若從近期表現來看，通訊債已有回升跡
象。8 月通訊產業債報酬率 0.9%， 高於美國投等債0.1%，
並為主要產業中表現最佳。

圖 3  8 月以來，通訊債報酬率領先主要產業

資料來源：Bloomberg，2026/9/2

從 8 月以來 OAS 變化來看，通訊債利差已轉趨收斂，
尤其有線電視與衛星通訊收斂幅度較明顯 ， 顯示市場開
始重新評價目前偏寬利差。

圖 4  近期通訊次產業 OAS 普遍收斂

資料來源：Bloomberg，2026/9/2 
另一方面 ，目前通訊債殖利率約5.98%， 高於美國投等
債指數約 5.55%，也明顯高於過去 10 年平均 3.95%。
通訊債近期即使已有回升 ，但殖利率水準仍位居主要產
業前段，後續若供給壓力逐步被市場消化，偏高利差仍
有進一步收斂空間。

圖 5 美 IG 殖利率處高檔，通訊債殖利率水準居前

資料來源：Bloomberg，2026/9/2

▎電信業者現金流穩定，寬頻用戶續增 
從通訊產業債主要發行公司來看，AT&T*、Verizon*、
T-Mobile*合計占比近 4 成，對產業現金流與信用基本
面具高度影響。大型電信商具備訂閱收入穩定、現金流
可預期的特性 ， 並可透過成本控管 、AI 效率提升與資本
支出節奏調整支撐自由現金流 。 根據Bloomberg 預估 ，
主要電信商合計自由現金流 2026 年可望年增長 6.7%，
高於 2025 年的 4.8%。

圖 6 主要電信商自由現金流維持高水準

資料來源：Bloomberg，2026/6/18 
10 年平均: 
3.95% 
自由現金流量(百萬美元)

3

誠信親切、專業創新 週報 
電信商也持續布局寬頻業務， 支撐中期獲利與現金流 。
其中 ， 龍頭AT&T*消費者寬頻用戶預估 2027 年增至約
1,380 萬戶 ， 年增約9%，Verizon*亦預估增至約 1,130
萬戶，年增約 7%，顯示大型電信商寬頻用戶基礎仍在
擴大，寬頻業務成長亦有助強化中期現金流能見度。

圖 7 AT&T、Verizon 寬頻用戶持續增加

資料來源：Bloomberg，2026/6/18 
*上述相關標的僅舉例說明，非單一標的推薦。

▎通訊債槓桿可控，付息能力穩定 
除主要電信商外，從通訊產業債整體財務指標觀察，現
金流對債務的支撐正在回升 。 自由現金流占總債務比重
自 2023 年低點回升，2026 年第 1 季落在 14.6%，已
回到近幾年相對較高水準 ，反映產業以內部現金流支應
債務的能力改善。

圖 8 自由現金流對債務支撐回升

資料來源：Bloomberg，2026/8/31 
槓桿方面，通訊產業的淨債務/EBITDA 自 2021–2022
年高點回落後，近幾季維持在約 3.0 倍，雖較低點略有
回升 ， 但仍低於前期高點 ， 顯示債務負擔仍在可控範圍 。

圖 9 通訊產業槓桿低於前期高點

資料來源：Bloomberg，2026/8/31

付息能力方面，利息保障倍數雖低於 2021–2022 年高
位，但 2026 年第 1 季仍維持在約 4.17 倍，比前季改
善，在高利率環境下仍具備一定利息支應能力。綜合來
看，通訊債偏寬利差較偏向供給與市場消化壓力，而非
產業信用基本面明顯轉弱。

圖 10 通訊產業付息能力維持穩定

資料來源：Bloomberg，2026/8/31

▎通訊債估值相對低，利差具修復潛力 
在現金流、槓桿與付息能力維持穩定下，通訊債相對多
數產業仍處於便宜位置。從 10 年跨產業 OAS 百分位
單位:百萬戶

來看，通訊債相對美國投等債、工業、能源與必需消費
等產業，多數仍位於高百分位區間，其相對利差補償仍
處於過去 10 年偏高水準。與多數產業相比，通訊債目
前利差補償仍具相對優勢。

表 1 通訊債利差補償仍高於多數產業

附註 ： 表中每一格顯示左側產業相對上方產業的OAS 比率 ， 在過去10 年中的百分位位置 。 紅色代表0–33 百
分位，左側產業相對較昂貴；黃色代表 33–67 百分位，相對中性；綠色代表 67–100 百分位，相對較便宜。

▎可關注現金流較穩的電信債 
通訊債仍具配置價值，可關注現金流較穩、信用支撐較
明確的大型電信債 。 從通訊債主要投等發行公司來看 ，
AT&T*、Verizon*與 T-Mobile*在訂閱收入 、 寬頻業務
與自由現金流上位於相對較佳位置。其中， AT&*T 與
Verizon*以龐大用戶基礎 、 寬頻布局與穩定現金流支撐
債 務 體 質 ， 在 大 型 電 信 債 中 具 核 心 配 置 定 位 。T-
Mobile*則受惠服務收入與自由現金流成長，信用支撐
較明確。Meta*雖受 AI 與資料中心投資影響，短期自
由現金流承壓，但槓桿低、評等高，且帳上現金與有價
證券約 902 億，債務體質仍具支撐。Netflix*則以訂戶
規模、內容平台與低槓桿支撐債務體質，為媒體娛樂中
信用品質較佳的代表。綜合利差、現金流與信用指標，
通訊債仍具配置價值，核心可偏向大型電信債，媒體娛
樂則以高評等 、 低槓桿或自由現金流較佳的發行公司為
主。

## Document 424: 兆豐-6446 藥華藥-20260907

評等分級：買進（預期未來六個月股價漲幅超過+30%）; 逢低買進（預期未來六個月股價漲幅介於+15%與+30%之間）; 區間操
作（預期未來六個月股價漲跌幅介於 -15%與+15%之間）; 逢高賣出（預期未來六個月股價跌幅介於 -15%與-30%之間）；賣出
（預期未來六個月股價跌幅超過-30%）。

個股報告 
藥華藥(6446) 
ET 新適應症陸續取得藥證，為未來兩年成長奠定基礎 
投資評等 目標價 
逢低買進 $1,586 
前次投資建議：無

報告日期：2026.09.07 研 究 員：鄭師安

摘 要 及 調 整 原 因 
➢ 公司 2027 年獲利因 ROPEG 市佔率持續擴大而明顯成長，給予「逢低買進」投資評等，6
個月目標價 1,586元(45X 27(F)PER)，潛在上漲空間 21%。

基本資料 結論與建議 
2026/09/04 收盤價： 1,310 ➢ 公司 26Q2 營收 68.55 億元， YoY+90.3%，毛利率
94.23%，稅後淨利 25.3 億元， YoY+204%，EPS 6. 13
元，獲利再創歷史新高。 26Q2 毛利率較 26Q1 增加約
6%，主因為產能利用率提升及價格調漲。藥華藥目前營
收主要來自 PV 適應症，惟全球骨隨增生性腫瘤 市場於
2035年將超過 200億美元，公司未來營收成長可期。 
➢ NCCN指南推薦 ROPEG為 PV低風險及高風險患者的唯
一一線治療，有助於滲透率快速提升。另注射筆劑型於
2026/06 核准，隔月開始出貨，已有病人在使用，受惠便
利性及安全性，有望提升患者長期治療的依從性。 
➢ ROPEG 用於原發性血小板過多症(ET)之新適應症已分別
於今年的 5/12、8/24、8/29 取得陸續向台灣、日本及美
國的藥證。ROPEG的 ET 適應症之全球臨床三期試驗 已
顯示出良好療效及安全性，並具備長期治療潛力。 ET 適
應症的營收貢獻可望與 PV適應症看齊。 
➢ ROPEG 用於早前期原發性骨髓纖維化 (Pre-fibrotic/Early 
PMF)或低或中度風險 1 級的明顯原發性骨髓纖維化之全
球臨床三期試驗 (HOPE-PMF)，目前正持續進行收案作
業。該試驗為藥華藥於骨髓纖維化領域重要布局之一。 
➢ 兆豐國際預估 2027 年營收 327.6 億元，YoY+19.2%，毛
利率 90.9%，稅後淨利 134.5 億元，YoY+30.3%，EPS 
35.25 元，獲利因 ROPEG 市佔率持續擴大而明顯成長，
給予「逢低買進」投資評等，6 個月目標價 1,586 元(45X

營 運 分 析

藥華藥專注於新藥研發，產品線遍及血液疾病、肝炎、癌症、骨髓纖維化 
藥華藥(6446)成立於 2000 年，為新藥研發、生產、與銷售公司，主要股東
包括行政院開發基金、耀華玻璃、閎泰投資等。目前產品線共有 數款款新
藥，適應症遍及血液疾病、肝炎、癌症及牛皮癬。公司於 1999 年研發出新
一代長效型干擾素(即 ROPEG，品牌名 Besremi@)，並將歐洲的 MPNs 相
關適應症的權利授權給奧地利孤兒藥公司 AOP。ROPEG 治療真性紅血球
增生症(PV)已取得全球 50個國家之藥證。

公司目前員工人數超過 1,000 人，位於波士頓的創新研發中心持續運用核心
技術平台推進新藥研發，涵蓋創新 TCR-T 療法等創新技術，逐步建構多元
研發管線，並以推進研發「同類首創」 (First-inClass, FiC)及「同類最優」
(Best-in-Class, BiC)藥物為核心研發策略。 另 ROPEG 的主要製造廠在台
中，可供應每年 2萬人之用藥。

- 3 - 
個股報告 
26Q2受惠 ROPEG銷售持續擴大，獲利再創新高 
公司 26Q2營收 68.55億元，YoY+90.3%，毛利率 94.23%，稅後淨利 25.3
億元， YoY+204%，EPS 6.13 元，獲利再創歷史新高 。26Q2 毛利率較
26Q1 增加約 6%，主因為產能利用率提升及價格調漲。藥華藥目前營收主
要來自 PV適應症，惟全球骨隨增生性腫瘤(PV、ET 及 MF)市場於 2035 年
將超過 200億美元，公司未來營收成長可期。

在全球各國的銷售安排方面，歐洲市場係與 AOP 公司簽訂授權合約；拉丁
美洲市場與 Pint-Pharma GmbH 簽訂授權合約；加 拿大市場 與 Forus 
Therapeutics Inc.簽訂授權合約；美國、日本、韓國、中國、臺灣等市場則
由藥華藥及其各國子公司直接銷售。

NCCN指南推薦 ROPEG為 PV低風險及高風險患者的唯一一線治療，有助
於滲透率快速提升。另注射筆劑型於 2026/06 核准，隔月開始出貨，已有
病人在使用，受惠便利性及安全性，有望提升患者長期治療的依從性。

ROPEG新適應症為未來成長動能 
ROPEG 用於原發性血小板過多症(Essential Thrombocythemia, ET)之新適
應症已分別於今年的 5/12、8/24、8/29 取得陸續向台灣、日本及美國的藥
證。ET 現行治療選擇仍有限，部分患者對既有療法可能產生耐受性不足或
療效不佳之情形，顯示仍存在未被滿足之醫療需求。ROPEG 的 ET 適應症
之全球臨床三期試驗 (SURPASS-ET)已顯示出良好療效及安全性，並具備
長期治療潛力。ET適應症的營收貢獻可望與 PV適應症看齊。

另 ROPEG 用於早前期原發性骨髓纖維化(Pre-fibrotic/Early PMF)或低或中
度風險 1 級的明顯原發性骨髓纖維化(Overt PMF at low or intermediate -1 
risk according to DIPSS Plus)之全球臨床三期試驗(HOPE-PMF)，目前正持
續進行收案作業。該試驗為一大型、全球性多國多中心第三期臨床試驗，為
藥華藥於骨髓纖維化領域重要布局之一。

除前述臨床試驗外，公司也與全球頂尖的醫療機構及合作夥伴進行研究者自
行發起之臨床試驗 (Investigator-Initiated Trial, IIT) ，持續擴展 ROPEG 於
MPNs 領域外之新適應症，包括皮膚性 T 細胞淋巴瘤(CTCL)、慢性骨髓性
白血病(CML)等。
